- [Subservient](https://github.com/N3xigen/Subservient) - Fully automated subtitle fetching and syncing suite with Smart Sync/DRIFT Recovery, Python/Docker, actively shipping.
- [Subtitle Composer](https://github.com/maxrd2/subtitlecomposer) - A Qt-based subtitle editor for KDE/Linux that supports multiple subtitle formats and provides tools for timing, text editing, and translation.
- [Subtitle Edit](https://github.com/SubtitleEdit/subtitleedit) - A free, open source subtitle editor that supports over 200 subtitle formats, with tools for sync, translation, and conversion.
- [Subtitle Workshop XE](https://subworkshop.sourceforge.net) - One of the oldest and popular freeware tools for subtitle editing. Subtitle Workshop XE is a modern update that supports a variety of subtitle formats, offering spell check, timing adjustment, and format conversion in a user-friendly environment.
- [Subtitles](https://github.com/active-video/subtitles) - AV Platform MPEG DASH subtitles.
- [The ultimate guide to CCs](https://www.3playmedia.com/resources/popular-topics/closed-captioning) - Comprehensive guide covering closed captioning practices, standards, and implementation methods for video accessibility.
- [ttconv: subtitle/caption format converter](https://github.com/sandflow/ttconv) - Converts EBU STL, IMSC/TTML/SMPTE-TT/EBU-TT-D and 608/SCC into IMSC, WebVTT and SRT.
- [TTML/WebVTT Coding of audio-visual objects - ISO/IEC 14496-30](https://www.iso.org/standard/75394.html) - Defines formatting for text tracks webvtt or ttml.
- [Vtt JS](https://github.com/video-dev/vtt.js) - A JavaScript implementation of the WebVTT specification.
- [Web Video Text Tracks Format (WebVTT)](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/WebVTT_API) - A format for displaying timed text tracks (such as subtitles or captions) using the track element.
- [Web Video Text Tracks Format (WebVTT) (W3C)](https://www.w3.org/TR/webvtt1) - The W3C recommendation for WebVTT, a text format for captioning and subtitling web video. It defines the syntax for caption files used in HTML5 video.
- [Webvtt Py](https://github.com/glut23/webvtt-py) - Read, write and segment WebVTT caption files in Python.
- [Webvtt Ruby](https://github.com/opencoconut/webvtt-ruby) - Parser and segmenter.
- [whisper-vtt2srt — AI transcript WebVTT→SRT cleaner](https://github.com/jorcelinojunior/whisper-vtt2srt) - Zero-dependency CLI/Python tool converting Whisper WebVTT to clean SRT, fixing karaoke-accumulation and filtering glitches.
- [Whisper-WebUI — subtitle generation UI](https://github.com/jhj0517/Whisper-WebUI) - Gradio UI generating subtitles from files/YouTube/mic using faster-whisper; outputs SRT/WebVTT, translation (NLLB/DeepL), Silero VAD, diarization.
- [WhisperX](https://github.com/m-bain/whisperx) - ASR pipeline producing word-level timestamps via wav2vec2 forced alignment plus speaker diarization, useful for accurate subtitle cue timing.

### Audio Analysis & Processing

- [ac3esbrowser](https://github.com/virinext/ac3esbrowser) - GUI analyzer that displays syntax elements of AC-3 / E-AC-3 (Dolby Digital / DD+) elementary streams.
- [audalign](https://github.com/benfmiller/audalign) - Python package that aligns audio files via fingerprinting/cross-correlation/spectrograms and inserts silence to auto-sync multi-source captures.
- [audfprint](https://github.com/dpwe/audfprint) - Landmark-based audio fingerprinting in Python, Shazam-style, supports fingerprint databases of ~1M entries.
- [Audio Offset Finder](https://github.com/bbc/audio-offset-finder) - Find the offset of an audio file within another audio file.
- [AudioAlign](https://github.com/protyposis/AudioAlign) - GUI research tool for aligning/synchronizing audio and video recordings of the same event captured from multiple sources.
- [audioprint](https://github.com/ctoth/audioprint) - Python MFCC-based perceptual-hash audio fingerprinting library.
- [audiowaveform](https://github.com/bbc/audiowaveform) - BBC-authored C++ tool generating waveform data files and rendering waveform images from audio, pairs with FFmpeg for video overlays.
- [Chromaprint — audio fingerprinting library](https://github.com/acoustid/chromaprint) - C library and fpcalc CLI generating acoustic fingerprints from PCM audio, used for content identification and dedup in media pipelines.
- [EBU ADM Toolbox](https://github.com/ebu/ebu-adm-toolbox) - Config-driven eat-process CLI for validating and transforming ADM (ITU-R BS.2076) next-generation-audio files, building on libadm, libbw64, libear and libebur128.
- [EBU ADM Toolbox Documentation](https://ebu-adm-toolbox.readthedocs.io) - Official documentation for eat-process configuration, ADM validation profiles and processing blocks in the EBU ADM Toolbox.
- [FFmpeg Normalize](https://github.com/slhck/ffmpeg-normalize#examples) - Audio Normalization for Python/FFmpeg.
- [freelcs](https://github.com/mhartzel/freelcs) - Watch-folder server that auto-corrects dropped audio files to EBU R128 loudness using libebur128, FFmpeg, and sox.
- [google/audio-sync-kit](https://github.com/google/audio-sync-kit) - Google library and CLI for measuring audio/video latency and A/V sync using characterized reference audio signals.
- [gst-home-audio (Dolby Laboratories)](https://github.com/DolbyLaboratories/gst-home-audio) - Official Dolby GStreamer plugins: EC3 parser/decoder and Object Audio Renderer for Atmos metadata-driven rendering.
- [libfvad](https://github.com/dpirch/libfvad) - Standalone extraction of the WebRTC voice activity detection engine into a small, battle-tested C library.
- [loudness-scanner](https://github.com/jiixyj/loudness-scanner) - CLI over libebur128 that scans files for Momentary/Short-term/Integrated loudness, LRA, and true-peak, and writes ReplayGain tags.
- [loudness_ebur128 — C++ EBU R128 Loudness Analyzer](https://github.com/google/loudness_ebur128) - Apache-2.0 C++ implementation of EBU R128 / ITU-R BS.1770 with an EbuR128Analyzer providing gated integrated loudness, loudness range and true-peak dBFS.
- [MPEG-H Authoring Suite](https://mpegh.com/academy/mas) - Official toolset to author, monitor, analyze and encode MPEG-H Audio, with MPF and BWF/ADM import/export for immersive and personalized audio delivery.
- [Olaf (Overly Lightweight Acoustic Fingerprinting)](https://github.com/JorenSix/Olaf) - Portable C library for acoustic fingerprinting, runs on embedded devices and WebAssembly.
- [Panako](https://github.com/JorenSix/Panako) - Content-based audio search and fingerprinting system robust to noise and distortion, with stream-monitoring subapps, academic-backed.
- [R128 (NDR)](https://github.com/NorddeutscherRundfunk/R128) - GUI loudness measurement app wrapping FFmpeg/ffprobe for EBU R128, built by German broadcaster NDR.
- [r128x](https://github.com/audionuma/r128x) - macOS CLI and GUI that reports Integrated LUFS, Loudness Range (LU), and max True Peak (dBTP) per audio/video file.
- [seewav](https://github.com/adefossez/seewav) - Python CLI that renders audio waveform as an animated MP4 video via FFmpeg.
- [silero-vad](https://github.com/snakers4/silero-vad) - Enterprise-grade, lightweight pretrained voice activity detection model, widely used for speech/silence segmentation.
- [soundfingerprinting](https://github.com/AddictedCS/soundfingerprinting) - Pure C# acoustic fingerprinting library that since v8 also supports video fingerprinting, with spectral-profile bridging for silence/fade regions.
- [SparseSync: Audio-Visual Synchronisation with Trainable Selectors](https://github.com/v-iashin/SparseSync) - BMVC 2022 Spotlight paper and code for sparse-in-space-and-time audio-visual sync detection.
- [spectre](https://github.com/snuffpuppet/spectre) - Go audio fingerprinting library built specifically for syncing movie subtitles to audio tracks.
- [SpectroMap: Peak Detection Algorithm for Audio Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2211.00982) - Academic paper on a peak-detection algorithm for audio fingerprinting with implementation.
- [SyncSink](https://github.com/JorenSix/SyncSink) - Synchronizes media files sharing common audio by computing sub-second offsets to align multi-camera/multi-mic recordings.
- [Virinext Bitstream Analyzer](https://virinext.com) - Bitstream syntax analyser and validator for AAC, AC-3/E-AC-3, MPEG-2 Audio, FLAC, Opus and PCM elementary streams.
- [ViSQOL v3 — Objective Audio and Speech Quality Metric](https://github.com/google/visqol) - Google's full-reference perceptual audio/speech quality metric producing MOS-LQO scores, with C++ CLI/API, Python bindings and a retrainable SVR model.
- [waveform-data.js](https://github.com/bbc/waveform-data.js) - BBC R&D library for generating and manipulating zoomable waveform data, commonly paired with peaks.js.
- [wavesurfer.js](https://wavesurfer.xyz) - Interactive JavaScript audio waveform player and visualization library built on HTML5/WebAudio with a plugin system.

### Batch Processing & Automation

- [aws-batch-with-FFmpeg](https://github.com/aws-samples/aws-batch-with-FFmpeg) - Reference architecture running FFmpeg containers (ARM64/x86-64/NVIDIA/Xilinx) on AWS Batch with Spot compute environments and SDK/REST job submission.
- [batch-transcode-video](https://github.com/nwronski/batch-transcode-video) - Ruby gem CLI for batch crop-detection and transcoding video files across a directory.
- [BFI DataDigipres Transcoding Scripts](https://github.com/bfidatadigipres/transcoding) - Archival-grade cron-driven batch transcode scripts used by the BFI National Archive, focused on FFV1/V210 preservation workflows.
- [ff-batch](https://github.com/dudewheresmycode/ff-batch) - Simple CLI batch FFmpeg utility with presets (e.g. ff-batch -i /in -o /out -p 720p) for bulk transcoding.
- [FFmpeg Batch AV Converter](https://github.com/eibols/ffmpeg_batch) - Drag-and-drop FFmpeg batch converter GUI running parallel processes up to the CPU thread count.
- [FFmpeg-queue](https://github.com/uwutube/ffmpeg-queue) - Fastify + Redis job queue for dispatching FFmpeg transcoding jobs, Docker Compose ready, production-used.
- [handbrake-batch-transcoder-win](https://github.com/e2m32/handbrake-batch-transcoder-win) - Multi-threaded HandBrakeCLI batch driver with network-move retry, pause/resume and skip logic for bulk library transcoding.
- [How to decode a video (memory file / byte string) and step through it frame by frame in Python?](https://stackoverflow.com/questions/60558412/how-to-decode-a-video-memory-file-byte-string-and-step-through-it-frame-by-f) - I am using Python to do some basic image processing, and want to extend it to process a video frame by frame.
- [pytranscoder](https://github.com/mlsmithjr/transcoder) - Python batch/concurrent transcoder with YAML profiles, rule matching, and cluster mode for automated FFmpeg pipelines.
- [Tdarr — Distributed Transcode Automation](https://github.com/HaveAGitGat/Tdarr) - Server/node architecture for distributed library transcoding over FFmpeg and HandBrake with folder watching, health checks, plugin pipelines and CPU/GPU worker classes.
- [Transcodarr](https://github.com/reedylab/transcodarr) - Watch-folder FFmpeg transcode orchestrator for the *arr/Jellyfin stack, with web UI, task queue, and automatic library refresh post-process.
- [Transcode (bmhayward)](https://github.com/bmhayward/Transcode) - Tools to batch transcode and process videos.
- [Video Thumbnail Generator](https://github.com/flavioribeiro/video-thumbnail-generator) - Generate thumbnail sprites from videos.
- [Videogrep](https://github.com/antiboredom/videogrep) - Automatic video supercuts with Python.

### Color Grading & Correction Tools

- [aces-input-and-colorspaces (ACES/ASWF)](https://github.com/aces-aswf/aces-input-and-colorspaces) - Official ACES color space conversion transforms from the Academy Software Foundation.
- [ComfyUI-EasyColorCorrector](https://github.com/regiellis/ComfyUI-EasyColorCorrector) - AI-driven color correction node for ComfyUI using perceptual color-space algorithms with face-aware skin tone preservation.
- [DoVi_Scripts](https://github.com/R3S3t9999/DoVi_Scripts) - Drag-and-drop scripts wrapping dovi_tool for Dolby Vision: extract/parse/inject/edit RPU, P8 hybrid creation, RPU resync, and profile conversion to Profile 8.
- [FFmpeg 8.0 libplacebo Filter — Full Option Reference](https://ayosec.github.io/ffmpeg-filters-docs/8.0/Filters/Video/libplacebo.html) - Complete searchable option reference for FFmpeg's libplacebo filter, including the frame-analysis compute shader path for static-HDR10 and untagged sources.
- [hdr10plus_tool — Codec Wiki Usage Guide](https://wiki.x266.mov/docs/utilities/hdr10plus_tool) - Community reference with flags and FFmpeg pipe recipes for extracting, injecting and inspecting HDR10+ dynamic metadata in HEVC/MKV.
- [HDR2SDR LUT Generator](https://github.com/n9Mtq4/HDR2SDR) - Generates a 3D cube LUT for HDR-to-SDR conversion by comparing paired HDR/SDR video samples.
- [hdrfix](https://github.com/bvibber/hdrfix) - CLI tool for tone-mapping HDR screenshots/video to SDR using multiple algorithms (Hable, Uncharted2, ACES filmic, Reinhard) with exposure/levels control.
- [lut (ray-cast)](https://github.com/ray-cast/lut) - Single-header C++11 library for color correction/grading, embeddable in apps and engines.
- [LUT Explorer](https://github.com/2TallTim/lut-explorer) - Qt/C++ tool to visualize color grading LUTs.
- [lut-maker](https://github.com/o-l-l-i/lut-maker) - Browser-only WebGL/Three.js GPU-accelerated LUT generator exporting .cube and Unity PNG LUTs, no install needed.
- [lutgen-rs](https://github.com/ozwaldorf/lutgen-rs) - Rust CLI for blazing-fast interpolated LUT generation/application from palettes or reference images, batch-scriptable.
- [myLUT](https://github.com/profmitchell/myLUT) - MIT-licensed LUT generator exporting 3D LUTs compatible with major photo/video editors.
- [nuke-colortools](https://github.com/jedypod/nuke-colortools) - Nuke color science and ACES toolset including an ACES Output Transform node.
- [OpenColorIO command-line tools overview](https://opencolorio.readthedocs.io/en/latest/guides/using_ocio/tool_overview.html) - Reference for ociobakelut, ociocheck, ociochecklut, ocioconvert, ociodisplay and ocioarchive - the CLI toolkit for LUT baking and config validation.
- [OpenColorIO Studio Config for ACES 2.0](https://opencolorio.readthedocs.io/en/latest/configurations/aces_studio.html) - Docs for OCIO's built-in ACES Studio config, referenced by URI with no external LUT files, covering the ACES 2.0 transforms.
- [OpenColorIO-Config-ACES](https://github.com/AcademySoftwareFoundation/OpenColorIO-Config-ACES) - Official ACES OpenColorIO config generator (cg-config/studio-config), maintained by the Academy Software Foundation.
- [OpenColorIO-Configs: ACES 1.0.3 Config](https://github.com/imageworks/OpenColorIO-Configs/tree/master/aces_1.0.3) - Widely-referenced OCIO ACES config with Python colorspace definitions, industry standard for color management pipelines.
- [Qtff Parameter Editor](https://github.com/bbc/qtff-parameter-editor) - QuickTime file parameter editor for modifying transfer function, colour primary and matrix characteristics.
- [rawtoaces](https://github.com/AcademySoftwareFoundation/rawtoaces) - Converts camera RAW files to ACES container per SMPTE 2065-1, ASWF-backed.
- [smol-cube — Compact LUT Binary Format](https://github.com/aras-p/smol-cube) - C++17 compact binary format for Adobe/Resolve .cube LUTs using half-precision floats, aimed at game engines.
- [spectral_film_lut](https://github.com/JanLohse/spectral_film_lut) - Generates film-emulation LUTs from real film stock datasheets using a spectral simulation pipeline, Python with GUI.
- [vapoursynth-tonemap — HDR-to-SDR Tone-Mapping Plugin](https://github.com/ifb/vapoursynth-tonemap) - C VapourSynth plugin tone-mapping HDR video to SDR with Hable/Reinhard/Mobius operators ported from mpv and FFmpeg.
- [VideoColorGrading (ICCV 2025)](https://github.com/seunghyuns98/VideoColorGrading) - Academic paper and code for video color grading via automated LUT generation, ICCV 2025.

### Color Science & Histogram Analysis

- [A Gamut-Mapping Framework for Color-Accurate Reproduction of HDR Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/1711.08925v1) - Academic paper (Sikudova et al.) proposing a gamut-mapping framework for color-accurate HDR image reproduction.
- [ACEScct Encoding Specification](https://docs.acescentral.com/encodings/acescct) - Official ACES documentation for the ACEScct log AP1 grading encoding, including the transfer function math and its relationship to ACEScc and ACESproxy.
- [CICP Code Point Reference (libavif Wiki)](https://github.com/AOMediaCodec/libavif/wiki/CICP) - Practical ITU-T H.273 CICP tables for colour primaries, transfer characteristics and matrix coefficients as used when signalling colour in bitstreams and containers.
- [Colour Science for Python](https://www.colour-science.org) - BSD-3 Python package implementing colour transforms, appearance models and datasets, with an ecosystem including colour-clf-io for CLF parsing and colour-hdri.
- [colour-science / colour](https://github.com/colour-science/colour) - Comprehensive Python colour science library implementing transfer functions, colourspaces, chromatic adaptation and colour-difference models used in HDR/WCG workflows.
- [dovi_meta](https://github.com/saindriches/dovi_meta) - CLI that creates Dolby Vision XML metadata from an encoded deliverable's binary RPU.
- [dovi_tool — Dolby Vision RPU Metadata Tool](https://github.com/quietvoid/dovi_tool) - Rust CLI to extract, edit, and generate Dolby Vision RPU metadata (profiles 4/5/7/8) and plot L1/L2/L8 levels.
- [gamutthingy](https://github.com/ChthonVII/gamutthingy) - Color gamut conversion tool implementing chromatic adaptation and gamut compression mapping (HLPCM) with quasirandom dithering.
- [hdr-analyze](https://github.com/tinof/hdr-analyze) - Reads raw 10-bit pixels frame-by-frame, computes per-frame luminance, and generates HDR dynamic metadata .bin files with a verifier.
- [HDR-Multi-Tool — HDR10+/Dolby Vision Metadata GUI](https://github.com/jessielw/HDR-Multi-Tool) - GUI for parsing HDR10+ and Dolby Vision dynamic metadata from MKV/TS/MP4/HEVC inputs.
- [hdr10plus_tool — HDR10+ Metadata Extractor](https://github.com/quietvoid/hdr10plus_tool) - Rust CLI to extract HDR10+ dynamic metadata from HEVC to JSON, plot brightness graphs, and compute Samsung scene info.
- [libplacebo](https://github.com/haasn/libplacebo) - GPU-based video rendering library (from mpv's algorithms) with dynamic HDR tone mapping, scene-change detection, gamut mapping, and native Dolby Vision Profile 5; powers FFmpeg's vf_libplacebo and VLC.
- [libplacebo Options Reference](https://libplacebo.org/options) - Authoritative option reference for libplacebo's GPU video rendering: BT.2390 tone mapping, gamut mapping, contrast recovery and LUT sizing.
- [obs-color-monitor](https://github.com/norihiro/obs-color-monitor) - Vectorscope, waveform, and histogram monitoring plugin for OBS Studio.
- [OpenColorIO (GitHub)](https://github.com/AcademySoftwareFoundation/OpenColorIO) - Academy Software Foundation's industry-standard color management framework for color space transforms and display transforms, used by Nuke, Blender, Krita, and major VFX pipelines.
- [openrv-web](https://github.com/lifeart/openrv-web) - Web-based VFX viewer inspired by OpenRV, offering frame-accurate playback, CDL/LUT/curve grading, waveform/vectorscope/histogram, and A/B compare.
- [QCTools Playback Filters (BAVC)](http://bavc.github.io/qctools/playback_filters.html) - Documentation for QCTools histogram, vectorscope, waveform, and CIE scope filters used in archival video quality control.
- [scopes_plusplus](https://github.com/MindStudioOfficial/scopes_plusplus) - C++23 realtime video waveform/vectorscope/analyzer using ImGui, OpenGL, and OpenCL.
- [YUView](https://github.com/IENT/YUView) - A QT based, cross-platform YUV player with an advanced analytic toolset.

### Conversion & Format Tools

- [av1converter](https://github.com/framicheli/av1converter) - TUI batch converter to AV1 with hardware encoder auto-detection and VMAF quality verification.
- [Avidemux2](https://github.com/mean00/avidemux2) - Open-source video cut/filter/encode tool supporting AVI/MP4/MKV/MOV, with GUI and scripting support.
- [batchtranscode](https://github.com/five82/batchtranscode) - Dockerized bash batch-transcode wrapper around FFmpeg, always outputs MKV, for home-lab library transcode pipelines.
- [bmx — BBC MXF Read/Write/Transwrap Library](https://github.com/bbc/bmx) - BBC library plus raw2bmx/mxf2raw utilities for SMPTE ST 377-1 MXF creation, inspection and transwrapping, bundling libMXF and libMXF++.
- [ClairMeta — DCP Probing and Conformance Checking](https://github.com/Ymagis/ClairMeta) - Python library and CLI validating Digital Cinema Packages: KDM checks, VF-vs-OV verification, SMPTE ST 430-2 signature validation and tunable JSON check profiles.
- [convert-video-formats](https://github.com/vib795/convert-video-formats) - Go CLI on FFmpeg for batch conversion with quality presets, parallel workers, progress tracking.
- [DVRemux](https://github.com/erikoey/DVRemux) - MKV to MP4 remux GUI tool fixing Dolby Vision and audio codec playback compatibility on LG WebOS TVs, lossless stream-copy.
- [FF Multi Converter](https://github.com/ilstam/FF-Multi-Converter) - GUI File Format Converter. Contribute to ilstam/FF-Multi-Converter development by creating an account on GitHub.
- [ffcvt](https://github.com/suntong/ffcvt) - CLI FFmpeg wrapper with zero-config recommended encode settings, supports recursive batch mode over directories.
- [FFmpeg-remuxer](https://github.com/Akagi201/ffmpeg-remuxer) - Remux without re-encode, infers target format from file extension.
- [FFmpeg-webgui](https://github.com/rendiffdev/ffmpeg-webgui) - Containerized web GUI for FFmpeg with transcode job queue and built-in VMAF quality scoring.
- [matroska-demuxer (Rust crate)](https://crates.io/crates/matroska-demuxer) - Read-only Matroska and WebM demuxer crate limited to elements common to both formats, dual/tri-licensed Zlib/MIT/Apache-2.0.
- [media-converter (M1XZG)](https://github.com/M1XZG/media-converter) - Self-hosted web-UI media converter with GPU acceleration (NVENC/AMF/QSV), Docker-ready, supports batch resolution upscaling and audio extraction.
- [mkv-web](https://github.com/ilian/mkv-web) - Browser-based MKV remuxer using FFmpeg.WebAssembly and web workers, remuxes to MP4/WebM entirely client-side with no server or extension.
- [mkv2mp4](https://github.com/ryanxedi/mkv2mp4) - Cross-platform Python GUI for batch MKV→MP4 conversion, with fast remux when streams are compatible and auto-transcode fallback plus SRT→mov_text subtitle conversion.
- [mp4-remux](https://github.com/EthanScully/mp4-remux) - Remuxes via FFmpeg C API through CGO (Go binding), not a shell-out script.
- [OpenConverter](https://github.com/OpenConverterLab/OpenConverter) - Cross-platform media converter (FFmpeg+Qt+BMF) with GUI/CLI, batch queue, and runtime-swappable transcode backends (FFmpeg/FFTool/BMF), lossless remux mode.
- [Python-video-converter](https://github.com/senko/python-video-converter) - Python library wrapping FFmpeg/ffprobe with a declarative Converter().convert() API for scripting format/audio/video conversion pipelines.
- [r3mux](https://github.com/Deepcuts/r3mux) - Dead-simple batch script that scans a folder and remuxes MKV to MP4 via FFmpeg.
- [SevenConverter](https://github.com/SevenbytesSoftware/SevenConverter) - Windows GUI FFmpeg-based converter supporting H.265/H.264/MJPEG/XVID/MPEG2 with broad hardware acceleration matrix (NVIDIA/QSV/AMF).
- [Shutter Encoder Documentation](https://www.shutterencoder.com/documentation) - Docs for the professional FFmpeg-based conversion GUI, covering its encode/decode functions, subtitle generation via Whisper, upscaling, and raw FFmpeg argument passthrough.
- [SPX_Videotool](https://github.com/TuomoKu/SPX_Videotool) - Automated batch video conversion utility for Windows built on FFmpeg.
- [unifi-protect-remux](https://github.com/petergeneric/unifi-protect-remux) - Niche true-remux tool converting proprietary Ubiquiti UniFi Protect .ubv camera format to MP4 without re-encoding.
- [video2hls](https://github.com/vincentbernat/video2hls) - Small Python script preparing video files into HLS-playable segment sets.
- [video_transcoding](https://github.com/lisamelton/video_transcoding) - Ruby CLI wrapper around HandBrake/FFmpeg for inspecting and transcoding video, actively maintained through 2025.
- [wo-video-converter](https://github.com/thurti/wo-video-converter) - Offline browser-based video converter powered by FFmpeg-WebAssembly, no server upload required.

### Effects & Compositing Tools

- [@diffusionstudio/core — client-side video editing SDK (WebGPU + WebCodecs)](https://www.npmjs.com/package/@diffusionstudio/core) - Framework-agnostic JavaScript SDK for programmatic video compositing and editing entirely in the browser using WebGPU, WebCodecs and WebAssembly, with no render server required.
- [AkarinVS VapourSynth plugin (Expr, DLISR, CAMBI)](https://github.com/AkarinVS/vapoursynth-plugin) - VapourSynth plugin adding an LLVM-JIT Expr evaluator, PropExpr, NVIDIA DLISR/DLVFX filters and a CAMBI banding metric.
- [Anime4K](https://github.com/bloc97/Anime4K) - A High-Quality Real Time Upscaler for Anime Video.
- [BackgroundMattingV2](https://github.com/PeterL1n/BackgroundMattingV2) - Real-time high-resolution background matting requiring a captured background plate, achieving 4K 30fps on RTX 2080 Ti, companion to arXiv:2004.00626.
- [BasicSR — image/video restoration toolbox](https://github.com/xinntao/BasicSR) - Open-source PyTorch toolbox for image/video super-resolution and restoration implementing EDSR, RCAN, ESRGAN, EDVR, BasicVSR and SwinIR.
- [ComfyUI-VideoColorGrading](https://github.com/kijai/ComfyUI-VideoColorGrading) - ComfyUI node pack for video color grading, integrating into node-based AI compositing workflows.
- [enhancr](https://github.com/mafiosnik777/enhancr) - Electron GUI for TensorRT/NCNN frame interpolation and super-resolution, polished alternative to Flowframes.
- [frei0r — Free Video Effect Plugin Collection](https://frei0r.dyne.org) - Minimal single-header C plugin API plus 100+ video filters, sources and mixers, loadable by FFmpeg, MLT, Kdenlive, Shotcut and LiVES.
- [GPUImage3](https://github.com/BradLarson/GPUImage3) - Swift/Metal rewrite of classic GPUImage, GPU-accelerated video/image filter framework for realtime processing and machine vision.
- [GPUOpen-Effects (AMD)](https://github.com/GPUOpen-Effects) - AMD org hosting TressFX, FidelityFX-Denoiser, FSR, SSSR, SPD - real-time GPU effects libraries, cross-API.
- [Graphite](https://github.com/metroncorp/graphite-rs) - Rust/Apache-2.0 vector+raster graphics engine with node-based procedural design and layer compositing, motion-graphics support on roadmap.
- [MetalPetal](https://github.com/MetalPetal/MetalPetal) - GPU (Metal) accelerated image/video processing framework with real-time filter pipelines for iOS.
- [motion-anything](https://github.com/nexu-io/motion-anything) - Apache-2.0 chat-native/agentic motion graphics engine with canvas compositor and WebCodecs MP4 export.
- [Natron (GitHub)](https://github.com/NatronGitHub/Natron) - Open-source node-graph video compositor comparable to After Effects/Nuke, GPLv2, cross-platform, supports the OpenFX plugin standard.
- [Natron — Open Source Node-Based Compositor](https://natrongithub.github.io) - GPLv2 node-based compositing application with a 32-bit float linear pipeline, OpenColorIO colour management and OpenFX 1.4 plugin hosting.
- [OpenFX 1.5 Release — Color Management and GPU APIs](https://openeffects.org/new-openfx-1-5-release-streamlines-workflows-and-creative-applications) - Announcement of OpenFX 1.5: color-management APIs, DrawSuite, OpenCL/CUDA/Metal GPU rendering support and Windows ARM64.
- [OpenFX 1.5.1 Plugin API Reference](https://openfx.readthedocs.io/en/main/Reference) - Complete OFX plugin protocol specification covering actions, suites, clip preferences and coordinate systems for building host-portable video effects plugins.
- [openfx-misc — OpenFX Plugin Set](https://github.com/NatronGitHub/openfx-misc) - Collection of OpenFX (OFX) plugins - Merge, Roto, keyers, generators, retiming - compatible with any OFX host.
- [Practical-RIFE — real-time frame interpolation](https://github.com/hzwer/Practical-RIFE) - Actively maintained, deployment-focused fork of RIFE (real-time intermediate flow estimation) for arbitrary-timestep video frame interpolation.
- [QTGMC — AviSynth Wiki Reference](http://avisynth.nl/index.php/QTGMC) - Canonical parameter reference for QTGMC, the highest-quality motion-compensated deinterlacer, including double-rate output and FPSDivisor semantics.
- [REAL-Video-Enhancer](https://github.com/TNTwise/REAL-Video-Enhancer) - Cross-platform GUI app for video interpolation/upscaling/denoising/decompression using TensorRT and NCNN backends.
- [rife-ncnn-vulkan — RIFE interpolation via Vulkan](https://github.com/nihui/rife-ncnn-vulkan) - PyTorch-free, cross-platform RIFE frame-interpolation CLI using ncnn and Vulkan for concurrent CPU+GPU processing.
- [RobustVideoMatting](https://github.com/PeterL1n/RobustVideoMatting) - Real-time human video matting via recurrent neural network requiring no green screen, achieving 4K 76FPS on GTX 1080 Ti, companion to arXiv:2108.11515.
- [toucan — OTIO timeline software renderer](https://github.com/OpenTimelineIO/toucan) - Renders OpenTimelineIO timelines using OpenColorIO, OpenImageIO, OpenFX and FFmpeg, serving as a reference for wiring editorial timelines to colour and effects.
- [VapourSynth Python Reference](https://www.vapoursynth.com/doc/pythonreference.html) - Canonical scripting API reference for VapourSynth's frameserver, covering core.std, resize and text filter namespaces used in high-end encoding pipelines.
- [Video2X — ML video super-resolution & interpolation](https://github.com/k4yt3x/video2x) - Machine-learning video super-resolution and frame-interpolation framework (C/C++ v6) wrapping Real-ESRGAN, Real-CUGAN, RIFE and Anime4K via ncnn/Vulkan, with CLI and Qt6 GUI.
- [VideoShader](https://github.com/snakajima/videoshader) - Script-based real-time video processing for OpenGL/iOS, composes multi-effect GL pipelines.
- [vkdt — Vulkan node-graph media processing](https://github.com/hanatos/vkdt) - GLSL/Vulkan node-graph (DAG) processing engine for raw stills and video, running all processing as GPU shaders.

### Metadata Extraction & Management

- [Alturos.VideoInfo](https://github.com/AlturosDestinations/Alturos.VideoInfo) - C#/.NET ffprobe wrapper library that auto-downloads the ffprobe binary for video metadata extraction.
- [AM Viewer](https://github.com/DolbyLaboratories/AM-Viewer) - Audio Metadata Viewer. Contribute to DolbyLaboratories/AM-Viewer development by creating an account on GitHub.
- [aomanalyzer.io](https://github.com/pengbins/aomanalyzer.io) - In-browser AV1 bitstream analyzer for ivf/obu/webm/avif plus mp4/mkv/ts/mov, running locally with no upload.
- [AtroDAM](https://github.com/atrocore/atrodam) - Full open-source digital asset management system (GPLv3, PHP/Docker), API-centric with flexible entity/relation data model.
- [codem-isoboxer — Browser ISOBMFF Box Parser](https://github.com/madebyhiro/codem-isoboxer) - Lightweight browser ISOBMFF (MP4) box parser for inspecting fragmented and progressive MP4 structure.
- [EBU Tech 3293 — EBUCore Metadata Set v1.10](https://tech.ebu.ch/docs/tech/tech3293.pdf) - EBU's core media metadata specification with XML Schema and RDF/OWL ontology for broadcast asset description.
- [EIDR Documentation Guide](https://web.archive.org/web/2024/https://www.eidr.org/documents/EIDR_Documentation_Guide.pdf) - Guide to the Entertainment Identifier Registry covering ID structure (10.5240/...), the minimum metadata set, and how records relate across versions and edits.
- [ExifTool](https://exiftool.org) - Widely used CLI/Perl library reading and writing EXIF/GPS/IPTC/XMP metadata across image, audio, and video formats.
- [ExifTool](https://github.com/exiftool/exiftool) - Gold-standard metadata tool reading/writing 30,000+ tags across hundreds of formats including video containers.
- [expo-video-metadata](https://github.com/hirbod/expo-video-metadata) - Cross-platform (iOS/Android/web/Expo) library reading video duration, dimensions, fps, codecs, audio tracks, HDR, and aspect ratio via Mediabunny parser.
- [Extracting contextual information from video assets](https://medium.com/netflix-techblog/extracting-contextual-information-from-video-assets-ee9da25b6008) - For an improved Netflix user experience.
- [ffprobe-Python](https://github.com/gbstack/ffprobe-python) - Python wrapper library around ffprobe for structured video/audio metadata extraction.
- [h264nal — C++ H.264/H.265 NAL Parser](https://github.com/chemag/h264nal) - Modern C++ NAL-unit parser for H.264 and H.265 (h265nal sister project), with cmake+tests, dumping parsed NALs from Annex B streams.
- [h26x-extractor — H.264/H.265 NAL Extractor](https://github.com/slhck/h26x-extractor) - Python tool extracting NAL units from H.264/H.265 bitstreams, decoding type and content with JSON output.
- [hls-id3tags](https://github.com/lebougui/hls-id3tags) - Tool to inject timed metadata into MPEG-TS segments via ID3v2 tags for HLS in-band metadata.
- [hls-id3tags — timed metadata injection into HLS](https://github.com/jamesjieye/hls-id3tags) - Tools to insert ID3v2 timed metadata (TXXX/TIT2) into MPEG transport streams for HLS timed-metadata delivery.
- [hlsinjector — ID3 metadata injector for MPEG-TS](https://github.com/dusterio/hlsinjector) - Injects ID3 frames at specified timestamps into MPEG transport streams used for HLS, driven by a metadata file.
- [IMFTool — IMF CPL editor with IMSC subtitles](https://github.com/IMFTool/IMFTool) - Open-source editor for IMF (Interoperable Master Format) Composition Playlists with IMSC1 text-profile subtitle decode/render/preview.
- [inspector.js — TS Media Container Demuxers](https://github.com/epiclabs-io/inspector.js) - TypeScript demuxers/inspectors for MPEG-TS, MP4, and WebM containers.
- [IPTC Video Metadata Hub Recommendation 1.7](https://iptc.org/standards/video-metadata-hub/recommendation) - Current IPTC video metadata specification with XMP/EBUCore/JSON implementations, clip-level properties and mappings to MPEG-7 and NewsML-G2.
- [isobmff-inspector](https://github.com/peaBerberian/isobmff-inspector) - Small JavaScript/Node ISOBMFF parser aimed at debugging, exposing box fields such as moof/mfhd sequence numbers for fragment analysis.
- [Isoviewer](https://github.com/sannies/isoviewer) - GUI application to have closer look ISO 14496-12 and other MP4 files.
- [libvideo-metadata (Parrot)](https://github.com/Parrot-Developers/libvideo-metadata) - C library for serializing/deserializing video metadata for streaming and recording, ships vmeta-extract CLI, used for drone-footage telemetry-in-video.
- [M2amedia SCTE35 Dump for MPEGTS](https://github.com/m2amedia/scte35dump) - M2A Media's tool for extracting SCTE-35 markers from MPEG-TS streams.
- [MADAM](https://github.com/eseifert/madam) - Python digital-asset-management library for image/audio/video: store, organize, transform, FFmpeg-backed.
- [MediaInfo (GitHub)](https://github.com/MediaArea/MediaInfo) - Convenient unified display of the most relevant technical and tag data for video and audio files.
- [mediameta](https://github.com/Vaiz/mediameta) - Rust native-first media metadata extraction library (creation date, resolution) with optional external-tool fallback via feature flag.
- [metadata — Rust FFmpeg-backed media metadata parser](https://github.com/zmwangx/metadata) - Rust CLI (FFmpeg-backed) parsing and formatting media metadata for human consumption; MediaInfo/ffprobe alternative.
- [metadata-extractor](https://github.com/drewnoakes/metadata-extractor) - Java library extracting Exif/IPTC/XMP/ICC metadata from image, video, and audio files, Maven-installable.
- [mextractor](https://github.com/caniko/mextractor) - Small Python package for fps/frame-count/resolution/duration extraction, lightweight alternative to full MediaInfo binding.
- [MP4 Box Inspector (browser-local ISOBMFF visualizer)](https://peaberberian.github.io/AISOBMFFWVDFBUTFAII) - Client-side web tool that visualizes ISOBMFF/fMP4 box trees with offsets, sizes and parsed metadata without uploading the file anywhere.
- [Mp4analyser](https://github.com/essential61/mp4analyser) - Mp4 file analyser written in Python.
- [mp4analyzer — MP4 Box and Frame Analyzer](https://github.com/andrewx-bu/mp4analyzer) - CLI and GUI MP4 analyzer that dumps boxes as JSON and renders a frame timeline showing I/P/B frame types plus decode versus presentation order.
- [mp4viewer — ISOBMFF Atom Analyzer](https://github.com/amarghosh/mp4viewer) - Python ISOBMFF atom analyser with shell, GTK, and JSON output for inspecting MP4 structure.
- [MPEG TS Inspector](https://github.com/brendanlong/mpeg-ts-inspector) - A command-line tool for inspecting MPEG-TS files. Contribute to brendanlong/mpeg-ts-inspector development by creating an account on GitHub.
- [Mutagen](https://github.com/quodlibet/mutagen) - Python module for handling audio metadata.
- [OpenAssetIO](https://github.com/OpenAssetIO/OpenAssetIO) - Interoperability standard/abstraction layer between DCC tools (Maya, Nuke) and asset managers (ShotGrid, ftrack), studio-backed.
- [OpenSceneSense — AI video scene tagging](https://github.com/ymrohit/openscenesense) - Python library combining vision models and audio transcription with dynamic frame selection to auto-tag and describe video scenes.
- [OpenTimelineIO Documentation](https://opentimelineio.readthedocs.io/en/latest) - Docs for the OTIO editorial timeline interchange API and adapters, including per-object metadata handling for post-production workflows.
- [Pmd Tool](https://github.com/DolbyLaboratories/pmd_tool) - A pmd_tool is a command line utility that converts between different representations of SMPTE RDD49 metadata.
- [Pymediainfo](https://github.com/sbraz/pymediainfo) - A Python wrapper around the MediaInfo library. Contribute to sbraz/pymediainfo development by creating an account on GitHub.
- [PyProbe](https://github.com/Protinon/PyProbe) - Python library that parses video metadata via ffprobe or MediaInfo into structured output.
- [scte35-go (Comcast)](https://github.com/Comcast/scte35-go) - Go library for encoding and decoding SCTE-35 ad markers, spec-complete to ANSI/SCTE 35 2022b.
- [sprite-vtt-generator — seekbar sprite + WebVTT](https://github.com/binoy638/sprite-vtt-generator) - Node.js tool generating thumbnail sprite sheets and WebVTT files with automatic interval selection for HLS/DASH seekbar previews.
- [SRT Metadata Extractor](https://github.com/Eyevinn/srt-metadata-extractor) - Contribute to Eyevinn/srt-metadata-extractor development by creating an account on GitHub.
- [Symphonia — pure Rust media demuxing and audio decoding](https://github.com/pdeljanov/Symphonia) - Pure-Rust multimedia library with ISO-BMFF, MKV, OGG and RIFF demuxers, tag reading and a pluggable FormatReader/Decoder trait system.
- [Telemeta](https://parisson.github.io/Telemeta) - Collaborative media asset management platform for musicology archives, transcoding/annotating video and audio with rich metadata.
- [TMK+PDQF — Meta Video Fingerprinting Algorithms](https://github.com/facebook/ThreatExchange/tree/main/tmk) - Meta's video hashing implementation producing fixed-length 256 KB TMK hashes with two-phase cosine matching, plus vPDQ for clip and subsequence matching.
- [video-metadata-extractor-js](https://github.com/opensubtitles/video-metadata-extractor-js) - Browser-based, FFmpeg-WebAssembly powered video metadata extractor with chunked processing for large files.
- [video_sprites — Ruby sprite + WebVTT generator](https://github.com/jronallo/video_sprites) - Ruby gem generating sprite PNG sheets and WebVTT for HTML5 player seek previews, with optional animated GIF output.
- [videohash — 64-bit Perceptual Video Hashing](https://github.com/akamhy/videohash) - Python library computing a 64-bit perceptual video hash via collage plus wavelet hashing, robust to transcoding, resizing and watermarking, with documented limitations.
- [VideoTaggingTool](https://github.com/CatalystCode/VideoTaggingTool) - Angular.js web app for managing users, videos, and tagging jobs with an extensible metadata schema for video annotation.
- [Voice Data Extract](https://github.com/abinashmeher999/voice-data-extract) - A command line interface to combine text information from subtitles with voice data in the video. Provides a convenient way to generate training data for speech-recognition purposes.
- [VTCLab Media Analyzer](https://media-analyzer.pro) - In-browser tool that helps to analyze the internal structure of MPEG-TS and MP4/MOV files.
- [xk media library](https://github.com/chapmanjacobd/library) - Scan millions of files with FFmpeg and access the metadata as a SQLite database. Also, a CLI alternative to media browsers like Plex or Jellyfin.

### Non-linear Editing Suites

- [DaVinci Resolve Scripting API Documentation (Unofficial HTML)](https://deric.github.io/DaVinciResolve-API-Docs) - Browsable, searchable rendering of Resolve's Lua/Python scripting API, which Blackmagic otherwise ships only as a bundled README.
- [DaVinci Resolve Scripting API Reference (Community Wiki)](https://wiki.dvresolve.com/developer-docs/scripting-api) - Browsable Markdown mirror of the official DaVinci Resolve scripting API for automating project, timeline and render operations.
- [Diffusion Studio Core](https://github.com/diffusionstudio/core) - WebCodecs-based browser compositing/timeline engine with declarative layering, transitions, and keyframing.
- [FFmpeg-webCLI — In-Browser Video Editor on FFmpeg.WebAssembly](https://github.com/tejaswigowda/ffmpeg-webCLI) - Fully local browser video editor built on FFmpeg.WebAssembly with timeline trim, transcode, and GIF export.
- [Flowblade — multitrack Linux NLE on MLT](https://jliljebl.github.io/flowblade) - GPL3 multitrack non-linear editor built on the MLT framework with a film-style cut-out workflow and 150+ frei0r/LADSPA filters.
- [FreeCut](https://github.com/walterlow/freecut) - Professional-grade browser-based video editor with multi-track editing, WebGPU/WebCodecs support, keyframe animations, AI tools, real-time preview, and export capabilities. Runs entirely in the browser with no installation required.
- [Kiwi-Edit](https://github.com/showlab/Kiwi-Edit) - Open research framework for instruction/reference-guided video editing via natural language, with code.
- [MediaEditor Community (opencodewin)](https://github.com/opencodewin/MediaEditor) - Node-based blueprint non-linear video editor with 45+ filters and 70+ transitions.
- [MLT Framework — MLT XML Documentation](https://www.mltframework.org/docs/mltxml) - Reference for authoring MLT XML producer/consumer graphs, the declarative render format behind Kdenlive, Shotcut and Flowblade.
- [Motion Canvas — TypeScript Animation Framework](https://github.com/motion-canvas/motion-canvas) - TypeScript generator-function animation framework with Canvas2D renderer and a real-time editor for programmatic video/animation.
- [Olive video editor](https://github.com/olive-editor/olive) - Free open-source non-linear video editor with its own C++/Qt node-based engine and realtime preview, independent of the MLT framework.
- [omniclip](https://github.com/omni-media/omniclip) - Browser-based non-linear video editor using WebCodecs, fully local with no upload/cloud dependency.
- [OpenCut](https://github.com/OpenCut-app/OpenCut) - Free multi-track timeline video editor for web/desktop/mobile built with Next.js/React, no watermark.
- [OpenReel Video](https://github.com/Augani/openreel-video) - Browser-based open-source CapCut-style video editor with no cloud upload or watermark.
- [OpenTimelineIO](https://github.com/AcademySoftwareFoundation/OpenTimelineIO) - Academy Software Foundation project providing editorial timeline interchange format and API, industry-standard for NLE timeline exchange.
- [OpenTimelineIO raven — OTIO Timeline Viewer](https://github.com/OpenTimelineIO/raven) - C++ OTIO timeline viewer for inspecting OpenTimelineIO editorial timelines.
- [OpenTimelineIO Swift Bindings](https://github.com/OpenTimelineIO/OpenTimelineIO-Swift-Bindings) - Swift language bindings for OpenTimelineIO, enabling OTIO timeline read/write from Swift apps.
- [Revideo](https://github.com/midrender/revideo) - Zero-dep code-first video rendering engine in TypeScript, headless render via CLI/API, runs serverless.
- [Revideo — Programmatic Video via Motion Canvas](https://github.com/redotvideo/revideo) - Open-source fork of Motion Canvas adding server-side rendering API and templates for automated/batch programmatic video pipelines.
- [Shotcut](https://github.com/mltframework/shotcut) - Qt/MLT+FFmpeg-based cross-platform non-linear video editor, actively maintained with ~14k stars.
- [Twick](https://github.com/ncounterspecialist/twick) - Headless timeline model and SDK for programmatic/backend video editing pipelines.
- [VoidCut](https://github.com/timii/voidcut) - Free, open-source browser-based non-linear video editor with drag-and-drop timeline, local-only processing (privacy-focused), FFmpeg integration, and simple export. All processing happens locally in the browser.

### Quality Analysis & Metrics

- [A Low-Complexity Perceptual Video Quality Metric for SVT-AV1](https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3715675.3715850) - Mile-High Video Conference 2025 paper on encoder-integrated low-complexity perceptual quality metric for SVT-AV1.
- [AVrate Voyager — Open Source Online Subjective Testing Platform](https://github.com/Telecommunication-Telemedia-Assessment/AVrateVoyager) - Dockerized web platform for crowdsourced subjective image/video/audio quality rating, with stimulus pre-processing CLI tools and validated remote-test methodology (QoMEX 2021).
- [BAND-2k — banding artifact database and detector](https://github.com/zijianchen98/BAND-2k) - Dataset of 2,000 banding images plus a dual-CNN banding evaluator that vastly outperforms CAMBI on banding-quality correlation (SRCC 0.88 vs 0.18), with code and weights.
- [Benford-VQA](https://github.com/skythianos/benford-vqa) - No-reference video quality assessment using Benford's Law first-digit distribution plus perceptual features.
- [bitstream_mode3_videoparser](https://github.com/Telecommunication-Telemedia-Assessment/bitstream_mode3_videoparser) - Open-source parser tied to ITU-T P.1204.3 QoE model, academic VP9-capable feature extractor from QoEX 2020 paper.
- [Butteraugli](https://github.com/google/butteraugli) - Google's psychovisual difference metric library for images/video frames, outputs scalar score plus visual diff map.
- [BVQA_Benchmark](https://github.com/vztu/BVQA_Benchmark) - Blind (no-reference) VQA benchmarking toolkit comparing multiple models.
- [CAMP-VQA](https://github.com/xinyiW915/CAMP-VQA) - WACV 2026 caption-embedded multimodal no-reference VQA model for compressed video, with code and HF demo.
- [ColorVideoVDP: A Visual Difference Predictor for Image, Video and Display Distortions](https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3658144) - ACM TOG paper introducing CVVDP, a spatiotemporal luma+chroma visual difference predictor that models display geometry and photometry and outputs JOD scores, plus the XR-DAVID dataset.
- [CompressedVQA-AEV](https://github.com/sunwei925/CompressedVQA-AEV) - Full-reference + no-reference VQA for asymmetric-encoded video, QoMEX 2026 challenge solution using SigLIP2+Swin-B.
- [COVER — Comprehensive Video Quality Evaluator](https://github.com/taco-group/COVER) - CVPRW 2024 winner of the AIS 2024 video quality assessment challenge; tri-branch Swin/ConvNet/CLIP architecture doing real-time 1080p no-reference scoring with transfer scripts for YouTube-UGC, CVD2014 and LIVE-VQC.
- [DOVER — Disentangled Objective Video Quality Evaluator](https://github.com/VQAssessment/DOVER) - ICCV 2023 no-reference VQA model that outputs separate aesthetic and technical quality scores, with ONNX export and a DOVER-Mobile variant for CPU inference.
- [Evaluating Video Quality Metrics for Neural and Traditional Codecs using 4K/UHD-1 Videos](https://arxiv.org/html/2511.00969v1) - 2025 academic benchmark comparing PSNR/SSIM/MS-SSIM/VMAF/LPIPS and no-reference metrics (MUSIQ, FasterVQA, Dover, Q-Align) on modern and AI-based codecs.
- [FAST-VQA and FasterVQA — Fragment-Sampling No-Reference VQA](https://github.com/VQAssessment/FAST-VQA-and-FasterVQA) - Official code for FAST-VQA (ECCV 2022) and FasterVQA (TPAMI 2023), Swin-based no-reference video quality models using fragment sampling, with a multi-thread variant running ~14x real-time on CPU.
- [fssimu2](https://github.com/gianni-rosato/fssimu2) - Zig implementation of SSIMULACRA2 metric, ~23% faster and ~40% less memory than the reference implementation.
- [ic-metrics](https://github.com/Ludicon/ic-metrics) - Optimized C++ implementation of SSIMULACRA2/SSIM/MS-SSIM, roughly 25-30% faster via sub-score pruning.
- [ITU-T P.1203 & P.1204 Bitstream-Based QoE Models — Open Implementations](https://telecommunication-telemedia-assessment.github.io/bitstream_based_models) - Project hub for open source implementations of the ITU-T P.1203/P.1204 bitstream-based video QoE models, including code, datasets and validation resources.
- [No-Reference Rendered Video Quality Assessment: Dataset and Metrics](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.13349) - 2025 paper introducing new dataset and metrics for no-reference quality assessment of rendered/gaming video content.
- [nr-vqa-consumervideo](https://github.com/jarikorhonen/nr-vqa-consumervideo) - No-reference video quality metric tuned for consumer video capture artifacts (sensor noise, motion blur, shake) rather than compression artifacts.
- [NRVQA-NSTSS](https://github.com/lfovia/nrvqa-nstss) - No-reference VQA using natural spatiotemporal scene statistics, MATLAB regression model.
- [NTIA/ITS Video Quality Metrics (VQM/BVQM)](https://github.com/NTIA/vqm) - US NTIA/ITS source release of the Video Quality Model tools, including full-reference and reduced-reference metrics standardized in ITU-T J.144 lineage.
- [OpenQoE — open source client-side video QoE platform](https://openqoe.dev/developers) - Apache-2.0 QoE measurement platform: a JS SDK instrumenting five major web players, a Cloudflare Worker ingest endpoint, and Mimir/Loki/Grafana dashboards with prebuilt panels and alerts.
- [Photon — IMF validation library](https://github.com/Netflix/photon) - Java library and IMPAnalyzer CLI validating SMPTE IMF packages: AssetMap, PKL, CPL and track files, with support for IAB, ISXD, S-ADM, App 2E and App 5 ACES, plus s3:// inputs.
- [PXNR No-Reference Video Quality Estimation](https://github.com/Ericsson/PXNR-No-reference-video-quality-estimation) - Ericsson's sample implementation of the ITU-T P.NATS phase 2 no-reference video quality estimation module.
- [Q-Bench-Video](https://github.com/Q-Future/Q-Bench-Video) - CVPR 2025 benchmark for LMM video quality understanding across technical/aesthetic/temporal/AIGC axes.
- [QCTools Documentation](https://bavc.github.io/qctools) - QCTools (Quality Control Tools for Video Preservation) is a free and open source software tool that helps users analyze and understand their digitized video files through use of audiovisual analytics and filtering. QCTools is funded by the National Endowment for the Humanities and the Knight Foundation, and is developed by the Bay Area Video Coalition.
- [Quality of Experience in Streaming](https://medium.com/@eyevinntechnology/quality-of-experience-in-streaming-5c25355a4111) - In Eyevinn Technology's ambition to broader our sharing of knowledge we now expand this with addressing quality.
- [RankDVQA — ranking-inspired deep video quality metric](https://chenfeng-bristol.github.io/RankDVQA) - WACV 2024 project page for RankDVQA, a full- and no-reference deep VQA model trained with ranking-inspired hybrid training, with code and a distilled mini variant.
- [Software Tools](https://github.com/VQEG/software-tools) - VQEG's Software and Tools Website.
- [ssimulacra2 (Cloudinary)](https://github.com/cloudinary/ssimulacra2) - Reference C++ implementation of SSIMULACRA2 metric, correlates better with subjective MOS than PSNR/SSIM/VMAF per-frame.
- [SSIMULACRA2 Codec Wiki Reference](https://github.com/av1-community-contributors/codec-wiki/blob/main/docs/metrics/SSIMULACRA2.mdx) - Community codec-wiki technical primer explaining SSIMULACRA2 mechanics and score interpretation.
- [Subjective and Objective Quality Assessment of Banding Artifacts on Compressed Videos (CBAND)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08700) - 2025 paper presenting the CBAND no-reference banding metric and the LIVE-YT-Banding dataset (160 AV1-encoded videos, 7,200 opinions), usable as a differentiable debanding loss.
- [T2VQA](https://github.com/QMME/T2VQA) - ACM MM'24 text-to-video quality assessment model using LLM-based scoring.
- [The Challenge to Maintain and Translate Creative Visual Ideas to Everyone’s Viewing Devices](https://medium.com/@eyevinntechnology/the-challenge-to-maintain-and-translate-creative-visual-ideas-to-everyones-viewing-devices-a88e1a841439) - Discussion of what went wrong with the visual quality of a very popular TV show episode, and the challenge of maintaining creative intent when encoding and distributing dark, high-contrast content at scale.
- [Turbo Metrics Performance (Codec Wiki)](https://wiki.x266.mov/blog/turbo-metrics-performance) - Benchmarking blog on GPU-accelerated SSIMULACRA2 computation performance.
- [UVQ — Universal Video Quality (Google)](https://github.com/google/uvq) - Google's no-reference perceptual video quality model for user-generated content, scoring compression, content and distortion without a pristine reference. PyTorch implementation with FFmpeg-based frame extraction.
- [vapoursynth-cambi](https://github.com/sgt0/vapoursynth-cambi) - Pure-Rust VapourSynth plugin implementing the CAMBI banding detection metric without a libvmaf dependency, exposing window_size, topk, tvi_threshold and eotf parameters.
- [VFIPS](https://github.com/hqqxyy/VFIPS) - Perceptual quality metric specifically for video frame interpolation artifacts, PyTorch implementation.
- [video-quality-metrics](https://github.com/CrypticSignal/video-quality-metrics) - FFmpeg-based benchmark tool comparing VMAF/SSIM/PSNR across different encoder settings.
- [videoparser-ng](https://github.com/aveq-research/videoparser-ng) - CLI/API video bitstream parser (H.264/HEVC/VP9) extracting QP/motion/block-size stats without full decode, research-grade.
- [VQEG — Video Datasets and Organizations Index](https://www.vqeg.org/video-datasets-and-organizations) - Curated index of subjective/objective video quality datasets and consortia, including CDVL and Netflix open content (El Fuente, Chimera, Meridian).
- [VQMTK: Video Quality Metrics Toolkit](https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S2352711023001231) - Open-source cross-platform tool bundling 14 VQA metrics plus SI/TI spatial/temporal indicators, CLI or Jupyter web UI.
- [Vship (Vapoursynth-HIP-SSIMU2)](https://github.com/Line-fr/Vapoursynth-HIP-SSIMU2) - GPU-accelerated (HIP/CUDA) visual fidelity metrics library implementing SSIMULACRA2, Butteraugli, and CVVDP.
- [YoMo — dockerized active QoE measurement for streaming](https://github.com/lsinfo3/yomo-docker) - Containerized active measurement harness from Uni Würzburg that automates browser playback sessions and extracts QoE metrics (startup delay, stalling, resolution switches) at scale.

### Scene Detection & Segmentation

- [AutoShot](https://github.com/wentaozhu/AutoShot) - CVPR-NAS 2023 shot boundary detection model with a NAS-optimized architecture and the new SHOT dataset (853 videos, 11,606 annotations).
- [AutoShot — A Short Video Dataset and State-of-the-Art Shot Boundary Detection](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2304.06116) - Paper introducing a short-video shot-boundary dataset and model that outperforms TransNetV2 on subtle transitions, with released code.
- [av1-scd](https://github.com/Khaoklong51/av1-scd) - Scene-change detection CLI generating encoder force-keyframe configs, wrapping selectable backends (pyscene, vs-xvid, av-scenechange, FFmpeg-scdet, TransNetV2).
- [Comskip](https://github.com/erikkaashoek/Comskip) - Tool for automatically detecting and marking commercial breaks in recorded video content.
- [DSBD — Deep Shot Boundary Detection at Scale](https://github.com/melgharib/DSBD) - Spatio-temporal CNN for shot boundary detection with an accompanying dataset emphasizing gradual transitions.
- [intro-skipper — Automatic Intro and Credits Detection](https://github.com/intro-skipper/intro-skipper) - Jellyfin plugin that detects intro and credit sequences using Chromaprint audio matching plus silence and black-frame analyzers, emitting standard media segments.
- [Katna](https://github.com/keplerlab/katna) - Pip-installable MIT library for automated keyframe extraction (LUV color-diff), video compression, and smart image cropping.
- [LMSKE — Keyframe Extraction for Video Summarization](https://github.com/ttharden/Keyframe-Extraction-for-video-summarization) - Large-model sequential keyframe extraction pipeline combining TransNetV2 shot segmentation, CLIP features, and adaptive clustering.
- [MDLSeg: Parameter-Free Video Scene Segmentation via Minimum Description Length](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2503.01201) - Academic paper proposing a parameter-free scene-segmentation method claimed to outperform PySceneDetect.
- [MKV-Chapter-Maker — auto chapters via black/silence/scene](https://github.com/optio50/MKV-Chapter-Maker) - Automatically generates MKV chapters using black-frame, silence or scene detection and writes them via mkvpropedit without re-encoding.
- [MythTV: Commercial Detection with Silence Clustering](https://mizar.mythtv.org/wiki/Commercial_detection_with_silences) - MythTV wiki documentation of silence-clustering commercial detection, including why naive silence detection fails on animation and children's content.
- [PySceneDetect Documentation](https://www.scenedetect.com/docs/latest) - Canonical docs for PySceneDetect's CLI and Python API: content/adaptive/threshold detectors and FFmpeg/mkvmerge-based video splitting.
- [PySceneDetect Project Site and Documentation](https://www.scenedetect.com) - Official docs for PySceneDetect covering detect-content and detect-adaptive algorithms, statsfile score export, and video splitting via FFmpeg or mkvmerge.
- [PySceneDetect — Similar projects index](https://www.scenedetect.com/similar) - Official comparison page listing alternative scene/shot detection libraries and tools with notes on their approaches.
- [Scene Detection Policies and Keyframe Extraction Strategies for Large-Scale Video Analysis](https://arxiv.org/html/2506.00667v1) - 2025 paper comparing scene-detection policies and keyframe extraction strategies at scale.
- [Scene-boundary-detection](https://github.com/abramjos/Scene-boundary-detection) - From-scratch implementation of "Ridiculously Fast Shot Boundary Detection with Fully Convolutional Neural Networks.".
- [Scenecut Extractor](https://github.com/slhck/scenecut-extractor) - Extract scenecuts from video files using FFmpeg.
- [SceneSeg (Local-to-Global Scene Segmentation)](https://github.com/AnyiRao/SceneSeg) - CVPR 2020 multimodal movie scene segmentation using place and audio features on the MovieNet dataset; reference ML baseline with code.
- [SceneSegmentation-SCRL](https://github.com/TencentYoutuResearch/SceneSegmentation-SCRL) - Official PyTorch implementation of CVPR 2022 Scene Consistency Representation Learning for movie-level scene segmentation (MovieNet/SceneSeg318).
- [Shot Detection Benchmarks](https://github.com/albanie/shot-detection-benchmarks) - A comparison of FFmpeg, Shotdetect and PySceneDetect for shot transition detection.
- [shot-boundary-detection](https://github.com/rsomani95/shot-boundary-detection) - Survey and implementations of multiple shot-boundary detection techniques including hard-cuts, fades, and neural-net methods.
- [ShotBoundaryDetection](https://github.com/ShidiDaisy/ShotBoundaryDetection) - Simple OpenCV-based scene boundary/shot transition detector, minimal dependencies, no deep learning framework needed.
- [ShotDetection](https://github.com/yasinyildirim/ShotDetection) - C++/OpenCV LGPL shot boundary detection tool, outputs frame numbers/durations in XML/YAML/text.
- [TransNetV2 — Deep Shot Boundary Detector](https://github.com/soCzech/TransNetV2) - Deep 3D-CNN shot boundary/scene-cut detector, SOTA on ClipShots/BBC/RAI with real-time inference.
- [Using FFmpeg's blackdetect Filter to Identify Commercial Blocks](https://blog.gdeltproject.org/using-ffmpegs-blackdetect-filter-to-identify-commercial-blocks) - Empirical evaluation of the FFmpeg blackdetect filter for locating ad breaks at TV-news scale, including concrete failure modes.
- [vhh-sbd](https://pypi.org/project/vhh-sbd/1.0.0) - PyTorch shot boundary detector from AIT's Visual History of the Holocaust project, focused on abrupt transitions in archival footage.
- [VidChapters-7M (Vid2Seq)](https://github.com/antoyang/VidChapters) - NeurIPS 2023 dataset-and-benchmark project plus Vid2Seq model for automatic video chapter generation at scale.
- [video-black-frame-detection — black/scene boundary detector](https://github.com/hasanhalacli/video-black-frame-detection) - FFmpeg + OpenCV black-frame detector for scene/ad-break/chapter boundaries, with optional LLM analysis and JSON/SRT output via REST API or CLI.
- [video-keyframe-detector](https://github.com/joelibaceta/video-keyframe-detector) - Lightweight Python CLI extracting keyframes via peak estimation on frame-difference signal, no ML dependencies.
- [video-scene-detection (IVI.ru)](https://github.com/ivi-ru/video-scene-detection) - Optimal Sequential Grouping algorithm implementation grouping shots into semantic scenes.

### Subtitle & Caption Tools

- [2srt2ass-cpp](https://github.com/mcourteaux/2srt2ass-cpp) - Merges a dual-language SRT pair into a single ASS subtitle file with encoding conversion and auto-sync.
- [alass — Automatic Language-Agnostic Subtitle Synchronization](https://github.com/kaegi/alass) - Rust CLI tool for automatic subtitle sync handling offset drift, ad-break splits, and framerate differences, 88-98% good-sync rate.
- [AutoSub](https://github.com/abhirooptalasila/AutoSub) - A CLI application to generate subtitle file (.srt) for any video file using Mozilla DeepSpeech.
- [Autosub (BingLingGroup)](https://github.com/BingLingGroup/autosub) - Command-line utility to transcribe/translate from video/audio/subtitles to subtitles.
- [AutoSubSync](https://github.com/denizsafak/AutoSubSync) - Subtitle auto-sync GUI wrapping ffsubsync/autosubsync/alass; can sync against a reference subtitle instead of the video.
- [CapMate — Automated Subtitle and Caption QC](https://www.veneratech.com/capmate) - Automated caption QC system validating SCC, TTML, SMPTE-TT, IMSC, EBU-STL, WebVTT, iTT and PAC files via a REST API.
- [captions — Lightweight JS Caption Parser/Renderer](https://github.com/vidstack/captions) - Modern ~5kB JS library parsing and rendering VTT/SRT/SSA captions, with server-side support, VTT-region rollup captions, and CSS styling.
- [CCExtractor Command Line Usage Reference](https://ccextractor.org/public/general/command_line_usage) - Official CCExtractor CLI reference covering --codec dvbsub/teletext, timing flags like -startat/-endat/-delay, -fixptsjumps and DVB subtitle debugging.
- [Cea Extractor](https://github.com/Comcast/cea-extractor) - Parsing and display logic for CEA-608 caption data in fragmented MP4 files.
- [Extract Subtitles](https://github.com/shawnsky/extract-subtitles) - Utility to extract subtitle streams from video containers into separate text-based subtitle files.
- [Eyevinn auto-subtitles](https://github.com/Eyevinn/auto-subtitles) - Whisper-based automatic subtitle generation tool that chunks large audio and outputs VTT/SRT/JSON while preserving sync.
- [faster-whisper — CTranslate2 Whisper Reimplementation](https://github.com/SYSTRAN/faster-whisper) - Whisper speech recognition reimplemented on CTranslate2 for roughly 4x faster transcription with lower memory, the standard backend for captioning pipelines.
- [FFmpeg 8.0 whisper Filter Documentation](https://ayosec.github.io/ffmpeg-filters-docs/8.0/Filters/Audio/whisper.html) - Reference for FFmpeg 8.0's built-in Whisper ASR filter: model selection, VAD, queueing, and SRT/JSON transcript output destinations.
- [Node Thumbnails Webvtt](https://github.com/estliberitas/node-thumbnails-webvtt) - Video thumbnail generator generating WebVTT spec file.
- [sccyou](https://github.com/amiaopensource/sccyou) - FFmpeg readeia608-based script that extracts SCC (and SRT) captions from SD line-21 video for archival workflows.
- [Serverless Subtitles](https://github.com/awslabs/serverless-subtitles) - Can handle a video input, extract the sound, transcript it and generate different subtitle files for your video.
- [subgen — Whisper Auto-Subtitles for Media Servers](https://github.com/McCloudS/subgen) - Auto-subtitle generator using Whisper (stable-ts + faster-whisper) for Jellyfin/Plex/Emby/Bazarr, CPU or CUDA.
- [Subs Extract](https://github.com/cessen/subs_extract) - Extracts per-sentence subtitles + audio from a subtitle file + video file.
- [subsai](https://github.com/absadiki/subsai) - Subtitle generation tool with WebUI, CLI, and Python API using faster-whisper; exports srt/ass/ssa/sub/JSON/txt/vtt with translation support.
- [subtitle-translator](https://github.com/rockbenben/subtitle-translator) - Batch subtitle translation tool supporting .srt/.ass/.vtt/.lrc formats with 17+ LLM providers, bundled format conversion, actively maintained.
- [subtp](https://github.com/mochi-neko/subtp) - Rust parser for SRT and WebVTT with round-trip parse/render of cues, comments, styles, and regions.
- [TV Subtitle Extraction](https://github.com/statsbiblioteket/tv-subtitle-extraction) - System for extraction of subtitles from TV broadcasts.
- [whisper-subtitle-generator](https://github.com/giakinh0823/whisper-subtitle-generator) - Cross-platform GUI for batch subtitle generation with SRT/VTT/JSON export, translation, and GPU acceleration. MIT.
- [whisper-subtitles](https://github.com/timoil/whisper-subtitles) - Accessibility-focused local subtitle generation using Whisper+OpenVINO, targeting hearing-impaired users across 99 languages.
- [WhisperLiveKit — Real-Time Streaming Transcription and Diarization](https://github.com/QuentinFuxa/WhisperLiveKit) - Live captioning backend implementing Simul-Whisper AlignAtt and LocalAgreement policies with streaming speaker diarization for multi-user sessions.
- [WhisperTimeSync — Transcript-to-Whisper Subtitle Aligner](https://github.com/EtienneAb3d/WhisperTimeSync) - Aligns an existing accurate transcript over Whisper timestamps to produce SRT with both good text and good timing.
- [whisperX-silero — Subtitle Generation with Silero VAD and ASS Output](https://github.com/cnbeining/whisperX-silero) - WhisperX fork using Silero VAD that emits .ass subtitles with character-level timestamps and per-language alignment models.

### VMAF PSNR SSIM Tools

- [AVT-VQDB-UHD-1-NVC](https://github.com/Telecommunication-Telemedia-Assessment/AVT-VQDB-UHD-1-NVC) - Dataset and code for 4K/UHD neural-video-codec quality evaluation, comparing VMAF, AVQBits, FasterVQA, CVQA-FR, LPIPS metrics.
- [Codec Wiki: XPSNR Metric Guide](https://github.com/av1-community-contributors/codec-wiki/blob/main/docs/metrics/XPSNR.mdx) - Community codec wiki page on XPSNR usage: the FFmpeg 7.0 HHI filter, (4*Y+U+V)/6 plane weighting, and the ~42 dB visually-lossless threshold.
- [Collection of VMAF Resources](https://streaminglearningcenter.com/blogs/collection-of-vmaf-resources.html) - A colleague asked for some resources relating to VMAF. Rather than answer in an email I thought I would create a post around it. Some of these are from Netflix, most from me (Jan Ozer). I've broken the items into three groups; Computing VMAF, Using VMAF, and About VMAF.
- [ColorVideoVDP — color spatiotemporal visible difference predictor (Cambridge Rainbow Group)](https://www.cl.cam.ac.uk/research/rainbow/projects/colorvideovdp) - Project page for ColorVideoVDP, a full-reference perceptual video quality metric modeling chromatic and spatiotemporal contrast sensitivity, outputting JOD scores plus distortion maps and "distograms"; differentiable so it can be used as a training loss.
- [ColorVideoVDP — reference implementation](https://github.com/gfxdisp/ColorVideoVDP) - PyTorch implementation of the ColorVideoVDP full-reference metric (SIGGRAPH 2024), usable as a CLI quality tool or as a differentiable perceptual loss for training encoders and enhancement models.
- [dynamic-crf](https://github.com/terranvigil/dynamic-crf) - Go CLI performing target-VMAF CRF search via bisection/interpolation for per-scene variable-CRF encoding.
- [easyVmaf](https://github.com/gdavila/easyVmaf) - Python script to easily compute VMAF using FFmpeg. It allows to deinterlace, scale and sync Ref and Distorted video automatically.
- [FFMetrics](https://github.com/fifonik/FFMetrics) - Windows GUI that visualizes PSNR, SSIM, XPSNR, and VMAF computed via FFmpeg with interactive graphs and batch processing.
- [FFmpeg Quality Metrics](https://github.com/slhck/ffmpeg-quality-metrics) - Calculate quality metrics with FFmpeg (SSIM, PSNR, VMAF).
- [MSU Video Quality Measurement Tool (VQMT)](https://videoprocessing.ai/vqmt) - MSU Graphics & Media Lab's tool suite computing dozens of full-reference and no-reference video quality metrics, with fast SSIM/MS-SSIM/VMAF implementations and CLI batch mode.
- [ssimulacra2_rs](https://github.com/rust-av/ssimulacra2) - Rust implementation of the SSIMULACRA2 perceptual quality metric, among the first with video input support.
- [sxpsnr](https://github.com/gianni-rosato/sxpsnr) - Standalone XPSNR CLI computing the perceptually-weighted PSNR metric between two YUV/Y4M sources.
- [turbo-metrics](https://github.com/Gui-Yom/turbo-metrics) - CUDA GPU-accelerated video quality metrics: XPSNR, Butteraugli, VMAF, and CAMBI banding detection.
- [VMAF-torch](https://github.com/alvitrioliks/VMAF-torch) - PyTorch reimplementation of VMAF metric, GPU/gradient-friendly for use in learned codec optimization loops.
- [Vship — GPU visual fidelity metrics](https://github.com/Line-fr/Vship) - GPU-accelerated (HIP/CUDA) implementation of visual quality metrics SSIMULACRA2, Butteraugli and ColorVideoVDP for fast perceptual video comparison.
- [What's new in AVQT (Advanced Video Quality Tool) — WWDC22](https://developer.apple.com/videos/play/wwdc2022/10149) - Apple session covering AVQT, a full-reference perceptual video quality tool producing MOS-scale scores with per-scene evaluation and HDR support.

### Video Analytics & Benchmarking

- [2BiVQA: Double Bi-LSTM No-Reference Video Quality Assessment](https://github.com/atelili/2BiVQA) - No-reference VQA model for UGC video using double Bi-LSTM, companion code to arXiv:2208.14774.
- [5G QoE Prediction: ML-based Quality-Shift Prediction for Video Streaming](https://github.com/razaulmustafa852/5g-qoe-prediction) - Machine-learning quality-shift prediction for video streaming over 5G networks, includes 1-sec granularity CLM/YouTube QoE dataset, companion to arXiv:2504.17938.
- [aomanalyzer — AV1/VP9 bitstream analyzer](https://github.com/xiph/aomanalyzer) - Xiph's Electron/web bitstream analyzer visualizing AV1 and VP9 block partitions, modes, motion vectors and residuals.
- [BsrVideoAnalyzer](https://github.com/JieHe96/BsrVideoAnalyzer) - FFmpeg+SDL based video bitstream analyzer with macroblock/CTU type overlay, I/P/B frame navigation, and bitstream header offset inspection.
- [codecbench](https://github.com/vigata/codecbench) - Config-matrix codec benchmarking tool that sweeps every setting combination and writes bitstreams plus quality metrics per run.
- [CompressedVQA: Full/No-Reference VQA for Compressed UGC Video](https://github.com/sunwei925/CompressedVQA) - ICME 2021 grand challenge-winning full-reference and no-reference video quality models for compressed user-generated content.
- [DVB Inspector](https://sourceforge.net/projects/dvbinspector) - Download DVB Inspector for free. DVB transport stream analyzer written in java. DVB Inspector is a java program to analyze captured DVB-streams. It is not a real time tool that can interact with PC-TV DVB hardware directly.
- [Elecard Stream Analyzer](https://www.elecard.com/products/video-analysis/stream-analyzer) - Professional Video Analysis Tool For Syntax Analysis Of Encoded Media Streams. Operates With MPEG-2 PS/TS, VES and MP4 Files. Automation, batch execution via Command Line Interface. Check your multiplexer.
- [Encoder-Quantization-Motion (EQM) Video Quality Metric](https://arxiv.org/html/2404.06620) - Academic paper on an encoder-side video quality metric for low-latency use, addressing gaps in VMAF/P.1204.3 via contrast/motion masking and MV-saliency-weighted quantization features.
- [Evaluating Video Quality Metrics for Neural and Traditional Codecs (4K/UHD-1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00969) - Academic comparison of VMAF, AVQBits, and FasterVQA no-reference quality metrics on 4K content.
- [GHVQ: Benchmark and Metric for AI-Generated Human-Activity Video Quality](https://arxiv.org/html/2411.16619) - Academic paper introducing a benchmark dataset and objective quality metric for AI-generated human-activity video, with companion code.
- [Gitl HEVC Analyzer](https://github.com/lheric/GitlHEVCAnalyzer) - Qt-based HEVC/H.265 bitstream analyzer with filter-based visualization of coding structures, motion vectors, and prediction modes.
- [google/rtc-video-quality](https://github.com/google/rtc-video-quality) - Real-time codec quality comparison framework across VP8/VP9/H.264/AV1 with RD-curve output for WebRTC scenarios.
- [HEVCESBrowser](https://github.com/virinext/hevcesbrowser) - Cross-platform GUI and CLI tool for analyzing HEVC (H.265) elementary bitstream syntax elements (NAL units, parameter sets, slices).
- [KSQI: Knowledge-driven Streaming Quality Index](https://github.com/zduanmu/ksqi) - Knowledge-driven QoE model for adaptive streaming video with implementations of multiple objective QoE models, companion to arXiv:1911.07944.
- [KVQ: NTIRE 2024 Short-form UGC Video Quality Assessment Challenge](https://github.com/lixinustc/KVQ-Challenge-CVPR-NTIRE2024) - CVPR NTIRE 2024 challenge dataset and code for short-form UGC video quality assessment, 600 videos/3600 processed clips.
- [MSU Super-Resolution for Video Compression Benchmark](https://videoprocessing.ai/benchmarks/super-resolution-for-video-compression.html) - Leaderboard evaluating AI upscaling models paired with codecs across 66 quality metrics and 1187 videos, measuring restoration quality of compressed content.
- [mv-extractor — H.264 motion vector extraction](https://github.com/LukasBommes/mv-extractor) - Extracts and visualises motion vectors and frame types from H.264 video in real time, useful for analysis and compressed-domain processing.
- [opentestbench](https://github.com/uclouvain/opentestbench) - Codec testbench running consecutive encode/decode passes with PSNR measurement and bit-flip error robustness testing.
- [Promising Initial Results with AV1 Testing - Streaming Learning Center](https://streaminglearningcenter.com/blogs/promising-initial-results-with-av1-testing.html) - I began testing AV1 early this week. Briefly, my tests involve 16 ten-second clips in four genres (movies, sports, animations, gaming) and an "other" category (music video, nature video).
- [video-decoding-benchmark](https://github.com/danilhendrasr/video-decoding-benchmark) - Benchmarking tool comparing NVIDIA Video Codec SDK, PyAV, and OpenCV video decode performance.
- [vmaf-cuda](https://github.com/michalkielan/vmaf-cuda) - GPU-accelerated VMAF using CUDA and NVDEC, up to 4.4x throughput and 37x lower latency at 4K versus CPU, now folded into VMAF 3.0/FFmpeg 6.1.
- [VQA² (Visual Question Answering for Video Quality Assessment)](https://github.com/q-future/visual-question-answering-for-video-quality-assessment) - ACM MM 2025 paper and code for VQA²-Assistant, a unified video/image quality scoring and interpretation model.
- [VQEG Software Tools](https://vqeg.github.io/software-tools/tags) - Video Quality Experts Group's curated index of academic/industry video quality assessment software tools and research collaboration hub.
- [WebRTC-vmaf](https://github.com/livekit/webrtc-vmaf) - VMAF benchmarking tool specifically for WebRTC codecs and real-time encode profiles versus source.

## Players & Clients

- [Advantech Video Solutions](https://www.advantech.com/en-us/solutions/video-solution) - Advantech offers a range of industrial-grade video solutions, including high-quality, real-time video transmission systems with UHD resolutions and low latency. Their VEGA series supports various protocols, aiding in broadcasting, mobile, gaming, and medical applications, accelerating the deployment of next-generation media solutions.
- [Agora Video SDK for Android](https://docs.agora.io/en/interactive-live-streaming/get-started/compile-run-sample-project) - Agora provides open-source sample projects demonstrating the implementation of basic and advanced Video SDK features for Android. These samples help developers integrate video calling and live streaming functionalities into their applications, leveraging Agora's global real-time communication network.
- [Agora.io Live Streaming SDK](https://www.agora.io/en/products/interactive-live-streaming) - Agora.io provides a live streaming SDK that enables developers to build interactive live streaming applications on mobile platforms. It offers features like dual video streaming, co-hosting capabilities, and adaptive bitrate streaming up to 1080p at 30fps.
- [AllPlayer](https://www.allplayer.org) - A free media player for Windows with support for a wide range of video formats and subtitles.
- [Amazon Kinesis Video Streams WebRTC SDKs](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/kinesisvideostreams-webrtc-dg/latest/devguide/webrtc-sdks.html) - Amazon Kinesis Video Streams offers WebRTC SDKs for C, JavaScript, Android, and iOS, enabling real-time, two-way audio and video streaming. These SDKs facilitate low-latency live streaming and data exchange between web, Android, iOS applications, and embedded devices.
- [Aniview Android Video Player SDK](https://aniview.com/mobile-sdk/android-sdk) - Aniview's Android Video Player SDK allows developers to integrate video experiences into Android apps, supporting multiple protocols like HLS and MPEG-DASH. It offers features such as subtitle support, customizable player UI, and monetization options through video ads, including support for VAST, VPAID, and VMAP standards.
- [Aniview iOS Video Player SDK](https://aniview.com/mobile-sdk/ios-sdk) - Aniview's iOS Video Player SDK enables developers to incorporate video playback into iOS applications, supporting protocols like HLS and MPEG-DASH. It provides features such as subtitle support, customizable player UI, and monetization capabilities through video ads, with support for VAST, VPAID, and VMAP standards.
