It develops technical standards covering all aspects of digital television transmission and reception in Brazil.
- [SDVoE Alliance](https://sdvoe.org) - A consortium of pro AV manufacturers, system designers, integrators, and technology managers working together to accelerate the replacement of point-to-point connectivity and the matrix switch with Ethernet-based AV distribution.
- [SMPTE 2110](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SMPTE_2110) - A suite of standards from the Society of Motion Picture and Television Engineers that describes how to send digital media over an IP network. It is intended for use within broadcast production and distribution facilities, emphasizing quality and flexibility over bandwidth efficiency.
- [SMPTE HDR, 4K, and Beyond Webcast](https://www.smpte.org/webcast-events/hdr-4k-and-beyond) - The Society of Motion Picture and Television Engineers (SMPTE) offers educational resources, including webcasts and courses, focusing on HDR, 4K, and advanced video technologies. These resources provide insights into the latest standards, implementation strategies, and technological advancements in the field of high dynamic range and ultra-high-definition video.
- [SMPTE ST 2084:2014 - High Dynamic Range Electro-Optical Transfer Function](https://pub.smpte.org/doc/st2084) - The Society of Motion Picture and Television Engineers (SMPTE) standard ST 2084:2014 defines the Electro-Optical Transfer Function (EOTF) for High Dynamic Range (HDR) content. This standard is crucial for ensuring consistent HDR video reproduction across different display devices.
- [SMPTE Standards](https://www.smpte.org/standards/overview) - The Society of Motion Picture and Television Engineers (SMPTE) is a globally recognized standards organization that has published over 800 technical standards for broadcast, filmmaking, digital cinema, and more. SMPTE standards ensure high-quality content delivery and interoperability across various media formats. Key standards include the Material eXchange Format (MXF), Interoperable Master Format (IMF), and the SMPTE ST 2110 suite for professional media over IP networks.
- [SMPTE Standards Projects](https://www.smpte.org/projects) - The Society of Motion Picture and Television Engineers (SMPTE) develops and publishes technical standards for the media and entertainment industry, covering areas such as broadcast, filmmaking, digital cinema, and audio recording. Their standards are essential for ensuring interoperability and quality in video streaming and encoding technologies.
- [SMPTE Virtual Course on HDR](https://www.smpte.org/virtual-course/hdr) - The Society of Motion Picture and Television Engineers (SMPTE) offers a virtual course on High Dynamic Range (HDR). This course provides in-depth knowledge about HDR technology, including its implementation, workflows, and best practices. It's designed for professionals seeking to enhance their understanding of HDR in the context of video production and broadcasting.
- [Streaming Video Alliance (SVA)](https://www.svta.org) - A global technical association that publishes best practices, specifications, and guidelines to address challenges in streaming video (e.g., open caching, QoE, ad tech).
- [Streaming Video Technology Alliance (SVTA) Projects](https://www.svta.org/svta-projects) - The Streaming Video Technology Alliance (SVTA) undertakes various projects aimed at improving streaming video technologies. These projects cover areas such as common token standards and streaming media tracing standards, contributing to the development of best practices and standards in the streaming industry.
- [Telecommunication-Telemedia-Assessment](https://telecommunication-telemedia-assessment.github.io) - A collection of repositories focused on methods, tools, and solutions for assessing telecommunication and telemedia technology. Key aspects include video quality models, databases, and tools, providing valuable resources for developers working on video quality assessment.
- [The GIMP](https://www.gimp.org) - An open-source image manipulation program available for all major platforms. It offers a wide range of tools for image editing, making it a valuable resource for developers working on video projects that require image processing.
- [uAVS3](https://github.com/uavs3/uavs3d) - An open-source and cross-platform AVS3 encoder and decoder. The decoder (uAVS3d) and encoder (uAVS3e) support the AVS3-Phase2 baseline profile. uAVS3d can be compiled for Windows, Linux, macOS, iOS, and Android, while uAVS3e can be compiled for Windows and Linux. These tools are valuable for developers working with the AVS3 standard, offering efficient encoding and decoding capabilities.
- [UCSHDRVideoComp](https://github.com/ratnajitmukherjee/UCSHDRVideoComp) - A novel HDR video compression algorithm that compresses HDR video frames into codec-suitable YUV files, compatible with 10-bit video codecs like x264, x265, and AV1. It focuses on uniform color space-based HDR video compression.
- [Understanding High Dynamic Range (HDR) by The American Society of Cinematographers](https://theasc.com/reports/understanding-high-dynamic-range-hdr) - The American Society of Cinematographers offers an in-depth white paper on understanding HDR, discussing the technical aspects and implications of HDR in cinematography, including dynamic range definitions and camera capabilities.
- [Unsupervised HDR Image and Video Tone Mapping via Contrastive Learning](https://github.com/cao-cong/UnCLTMO) - This project presents a unified framework (IVTMNet) for unsupervised image and video tone mapping, utilizing contrastive learning to improve the training process. It introduces a novel latent code to measure similarity between tone-mapped results and employs spatial-feature-enhanced and temporal-feature-replaced modules to enhance information exchange and temporal consistency. The project also provides a large-scale unpaired HDR-LDR video dataset, making it a valuable resource for developers working on HDR video tone mapping and related applications.
- [Use DirectX with Advanced Color on High/Standard Dynamic Range Displays](https://github.com/MicrosoftDocs/win32/blob/docs/desktop-src/direct3darticles/high-dynamic-range.md) - A detailed guide from Microsoft on utilizing DirectX for Advanced Color scenarios, including HDR and wide color gamut, providing instructions for rendering HDR content, adapting to display capabilities using tone mapping, and capturing HDR screen content.
- [UWA HDR Vivid Standards](https://uhd-world-association.com/uwa-resources) - The UHD World Association (UWA) has developed the HDR Vivid Standards, which provide technical requirements and test methods for various devices and applications involved in HDR video technology. These standards aim to ensure consistent technical indicators across the industry, promoting innovation, compatibility, and cohesive growth. The standards cover aspects such as system integration, post-production, display devices, player devices, and live encoder devices.
- [Versatile Video Coding (VVC) - MPEG-I Part 3](https://www.mpeg.org/standards/mpeg-i) - VVC, also known as H.266 and MPEG-I Part 3, is a video compression standard designed to achieve higher compression efficiency and versatility for a wide range of applications, including streaming, broadcasting, and immersive media. It supports resolutions up to 16K and various color formats.
- [Versatile Video Coding (VVC) Standard](https://www.mpeg.org/standards/MPEG-I/3) - Part of the MPEG-I suite, VVC (ISO/IEC 23090-3) is the latest video compression standard offering improved compression efficiency and support for a wide range of applications, including 4K and 8K video streaming. This resource provides detailed information and whitepapers on VVC.
- [Versatile Video Coding (VVC) Test Model](https://vcgit.hhi.fraunhofer.de/jvet/VVCSoftware_VTM) - The VVC Test Model (VTM) is the reference software for the Versatile Video Coding (VVC) standard, also known as H.266. VVC aims to provide significant compression efficiency improvements over previous standards. The VTM serves as a reference for developers implementing VVC in their applications.
- [Versatile Video Coding Standard: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2106.14245) - A comprehensive review paper detailing the Versatile Video Coding (VVC) standard, covering coding tools, consumer deployment, and implementation aspects. This resource is beneficial for developers and researchers interested in the technical details and applications of VVC.
- [VESA DisplayHDR Standard](https://vesa.org/featured-articles/vesa-defines-new-standard-to-help-speed-pc-industry-adoption-of%E2%80%A8high-dynamic-range-technology-in-laptop-and-desktop-monitor-displays) - The Video Electronics Standards Association (VESA) has defined the DisplayHDR standard, the industry's first open HDR specification with a transparent testing methodology. This standard specifies HDR quality levels, including luminance, color gamut, bit depth, and rise time, to facilitate the adoption of HDR in laptop and desktop monitors. It establishes three distinct levels of HDR system performance to cater to various market needs.
- [Video Electronics Standards Association (VESA)](https://www.vesa.org) - VESA is a technical standards organization for computer display standards. It has issued several widely used standards, including DisplayHDR, DisplayPort, and the Flat Display Mounting Interface. VESA's standards are crucial for ensuring compatibility and performance across various display technologies and devices.
- [Video Quality Experts Group (VQEG)](https://vqeg.org/vqeg-home) - International standards group focused on video quality assessment methodologies and research.
- [Video Services Forum (VSF)](https://www.vsf.tv) - VSF is an international association dedicated to interoperability, quality metrics, and education for media networking technologies. It provides forums to identify issues and promotes interoperability by contributing to the development of standards.
- [W3C Audiovisual Media Formats for Browsers Community Group](https://github.com/w3c/avmedia-formats-cg) - This GitHub repository is the official space for the W3C Audiovisual Media Formats for Browsers Community Group. The group focuses on discussing and developing support for modern audiovisual formats within the W3C framework, aiming to synchronize open and proprietary solutions to enhance web-based media experiences.
- [W3C Media & Entertainment Interest Group](https://www.w3.org/blog/news/archives/9075) - A World Wide Web Consortium group focused on media and entertainment. It examines use cases and requirements for web technologies in the media industry (like streaming, broadcasting integration, etc.) and often produces recommendations or liaisons with other standards (like W3C for HTML5 media, or CTA WAVE).
- [W3C Media Working Group Repositories](https://www.w3.org/groups/wg/media/tools) - The W3C Media Working Group maintains several repositories related to media technologies on the web, including specifications and tools for media playback, encryption, and streaming. These resources are crucial for developers working on web-based media applications.
- [xAVS2 & dAVS2](https://github.com/pkuvcl/xavs2) - XAVS2 and dAVS2 are open-source encoder and decoder published by Peking University Video Coding Laboratory (PKU-VCL) based on the AVS2-P2/IEEE 1857.4 video coding standard. These tools are offered under either version 2 of the GNU General Public License (GPL) or a commercial license. They are valuable for developers working with the AVS2 standard, providing efficient encoding and decoding solutions.
- [XEVD: eXtra-fast Essential Video Decoder](https://github.com/mpeg5/xevd) - XEVD is the counterpart decoder to XEVE, providing an open-source implementation of the MPEG-5 EVC standard. It enables efficient decoding of EVC-encoded video streams.
- [XEVE: eXtra-fast Essential Video Encoder](https://github.com/mpeg5/xeve) - XEVE is an open-source video encoder implementing the MPEG-5 Essential Video Coding (EVC) standard. It offers improved visual quality for video calls and addresses licensing complexities associated with other codecs.

### Adaptive Streaming Standards

- [astronautlabs/scte35](https://github.com/astronautlabs/scte35) - TypeScript implementation of SCTE-35 (2019 spec), part of Astronaut Labs broadcast tooling suite (also scte104, RTP).
- [AV1 Rtp Spec](https://github.com/AOMediaCodec/av1-rtp-spec) - Current draft (HTML): HTTPS://aomediacodec.GitHub.io/av1-rtp-spec/.
- [CMSD — Update on Implementations and Validation of Key Use Cases (Mile-High Video 2023)](https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3588444.3591031) - Paper validating CMSD use cases in practice: edge throughput estimation, prefetch hints and edge-suggested bitrate tested in dash.js.
- [CTA WAVE Project (GitHub org)](https://github.com/cta-wave) - Official CTA WAVE GitHub org: mezzanine test content builder, WMAS/DPCTF conformance test suites, CMCD reference repo.
- [CTA-5000-B WAVE Content Specification](https://cdn.cta.tech/cta/media/media/resources/standards/pdfs/cta-5000-b-final_v2.pdf) - Official CTA WAVE project specification for web media ecosystem interoperability across adaptive streaming devices.
- [ETSI TS 103 998: DASH-IF Content Steering for DASH and HLS](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/103900_103999/103998/01.01.01_60/ts_103998v010101p.pdf) - Normative ETSI specification of the content steering protocol used by DASH and HLS players to switch between CDNs and service locations at runtime.
- [FFmpeg-scte-35](https://github.com/lexxwork/ffmpeg-scte-35) - FFmpeg v4.4 patched with SCTE-35 support for practical ad-cue injection/passthrough.
- [Guidelines for Implementation: DASH-IF Interoperability Points](https://dashif.org/docs/DASH-IF-IOP-v4.3.pdf) - DASH Industry Forum's implementation guidelines document specifying interoperability points for DASH-based adaptive streaming systems.
- [HbbTV 2.0.5 Core Specification Published](https://developer.hbbtv.org/news/hbbtv-association-publishes-hbbtv-2-0-5) - Announcement detailing HbbTV 2.0.5 changes: DRM moved into the core spec, WebAssembly support, and optional VVC and AV1 codecs.
- [HLS Authoring Specification for Apple Devices](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/http-live-streaming/hls-authoring-specification-for-apple-devices) - Apple's official normative HLS content requirements covering codecs, segmenting, and HDR/VIDEO-RANGE rules.
- [httpie-esni-auth](https://github.com/istreamlabs/httpie-esni-auth) - HTTPie authentication plugin implementing SCTE-224 ESNI request signing.
- [ISO/IEC 23009-1:2019 — MPEG-DASH Part 1: Media Presentation Description and Segment Formats](https://www.iso.org/standard/79329.html) - ISO catalogue entry for a later edition of ISO/IEC 23009-1 (MPEG-DASH Part 1), covering the media presentation description and segment formats with subsequent amendments.
- [Low Latency Live Streaming Implementation in DASH and HLS](https://dl.acm.org/doi/pdf/10.1145/3503161.3548544) - Academic conference paper (Bentaleb et al.) detailing implementation specifics of low-latency live streaming across both DASH and HLS standards.
- [MPEG Common media application format (CMAF) for segmented media - ISO/IEC 23000-19](https://www.iso.org/standard/85623.html) - ISO/IEC 23000-19, the Common Media Application Format (CMAF) specification - a segmented media profile that lets a single encode serve both HLS and DASH delivery.
- [NAB 2025: Streaming Tech Trends Update (Will Law)](https://www.csimagazine.com/csi/NAB2025-Will-Law-streaming-tech-update.php) - Interview-based technical update covering CMCD v2 Response and Event modes, funded player integrations in Shaka/dash.js, and open specification issues.
- [OTT Resources: Low-Latency Streaming (curated talk & reading list)](https://github.com/thijsl/ott-resources/blob/master/low-latency.md) - Curated list of conference talks (Mile High Video 2019, Streaming Media East 2019) and articles comparing LL-HLS, LHLS, and DASH low-latency approaches.
- [RFC 8216: HTTP Live Streaming](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc8216) - Official IETF specification for HTTP Live Streaming (HLS).
- [RFC 9317: Operational Considerations for Streaming Media](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9317.html) - IETF doc covering HLS/DASH ABR rate-adaptation strategies and SCTE-35/224 signaling context.
- [SCTE](https://github.com/jamesfining/scte) - Python library to work with SCTE standards.
- [SCTE-35/SCTE-104/SCTE-67 reference](https://github.com/leandromoreira/scte-35-scte-104-scte-67) - Consolidated reference docs on dynamic ad insertion via SCTE-104/SCTE-35/SCTE-67 across HLS, DASH, Smooth Streaming, and RTMP.
- [scte224structs](https://github.com/Comcast/scte224structs) - Go structs and utilities modeling SCTE-224 (ESNI) ad-scheduling data, reference implementation from Comcast.
- [scte35-reader](https://github.com/dholroyd/scte35-reader) - Rust SCTE-35 parser library, ships with scte35dump example CLI tool.
- [The Real-Time Messaging Protocol Explained](https://www.wowza.com/blog/rtmp-streaming-real-time-messaging-protocol) - Explanation of the Real-Time Messaging Protocol, a protocol used for streaming audio and video content over the internet.
- [threefive](https://pypi.org/project/threefive) - Most complete SCTE-35 parse/encode Python library, supports MPEGTS plus base64/hex signal formats.
- [Toward One-Second Latency: Evolution of Live Media Streaming](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2310.03256) - Academic survey tracing evolution of low-latency streaming standards from RTMP through LL-HLS, LL-DASH, CMAF, and WebRTC.
- [W3C — Scalable Video Coding (SVC) Extension for WebRTC](https://www.w3.org/TR/webrtc-svc) - Normative spec for scalabilityMode values (LxTy, SxTy, _KEY), codec capability discovery and SVC constraints in WebRTC encoders.

### Best Practices & Guidelines

- [Apple Got It Wrong: Encoding Specs for HEVC in HLS](https://www.streamingmedia.com/Articles/ReadArticle.aspx?ArticleID=121878) - Adding HEVC to your HLS streams is looking like a no brainer, but if you decide to do so, you may not want to take Apple's HEVC encoding recommendations verbatim. You'll deliver noticeably higher quality video if you follow the advice detailed below.
- [ATSC A/85 — Techniques for Establishing and Maintaining Audio Loudness](https://www.atsc.org/atsc-documents/a85-techniques-for-establishing-and-maintaining-audio-loudness-for-digital-television) - The North American recommended practice for digital television audio loudness measurement and control, including loudness/true-peak quick reference.
- [Cable Bible](https://github.com/amiaopensource/cable-bible) - A guide to cables and connectors used for audiovisual tech.
- [Caniuse](https://github.com/Fyrd/caniuse) - Raw browser/feature support data from caniuse.com.
- [Dolby Vision for Content Creators](https://www.dolby.com/us/en/technologies/dolby-vision/dolby-vision-for-creative-professionals.html) - The Dolby Vision integrated workflow gives you the tools needed to efficiently create wide color gamut and high dynamic range content and ensures that the look you create in the color suite stays true when experienced across across a multitude of devices. With Dolby Vision high dynamic range imaging, you get bolder highlights and incredible contrast. That means greater sharpness, depth, and more detailed shadows to expand your storytelling possibilities like never before.
- [EBU R128 Introduction - Florian Camerer](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iuEtQqC-Sqo) - Florian Camerer gives an introduction to the European Broadcasting Union's R128 broadcast loudness standard, covering perceived loudness, peak normalization, and loudness range measurement.
- [EBU TR 045 — Codec-Independent Next-Generation Audio Workflows](https://tech.ebu.ch/docs/techreports/tr045.pdf) - EBU technical report arguing for open, codec-independent NGA production workflows and explaining the role of ADM in immersive/personalized audio delivery.
- [HLG vs PQ Systems for HDR Television](https://www.displaydaily.com/article/display-daily/hlg-vs-pq-systems-for-hdr-television) - Article explaining hlg vs pq in depth.
- [IMF: A Prescription for Versionitis](https://medium.com/netflix-techblog/imf-a-prescription-for-versionitis-e0b4c1865c20) - The emerging Interoperable Master Format standard.
- [SRT Cookbook](https://srtlab.github.io/srt-cookbook) - More in-depth technical documentaion on the SRT protocol and library.
- [Techniques for Establishing and Maintaining Audio Loudness for Digital Television](https://www.atsc.org/wp-content/uploads/2015/03/Techniques-for-establishing-and-maintaining-audio-loudness.pdf) - ATSC technical standard documenting methods for measuring, establishing, and maintaining consistent audio loudness levels in digital television.

#### HDR Guidelines

- [Android HDR Video Playback (AOSP Platform Docs)](https://source.android.com/docs/core/display/hdr) - AOSP documentation of the platform HDR pipeline: HLG vs PQ handling, mastering display and content light level metadata (mdcv/clli, SmDm/CoLL) plumbing, and Dolby Vision/HDR10+ support requirements.
- [Apple Tech Talk — Authoring 4K and HDR HLS Streams](https://developer.apple.com/videos/play/tech-talks/501) - Apple session on the tagging rules for 4K and HDR HLS variants, including VIDEO-RANGE signalling for SDR, HLG and PQ.
- [AV1 HDR10+ Metadata Handling Specification (AOMediaCodec)](https://github.com/AOMediaCodec/av1-hdr10plus) - Official AOM repo specifying HDR10+ dynamic metadata handling for AV1, with Bikeshed spec source and conformance test suite.
- [awesome-deep-hdr](https://github.com/vinthony/awesome-deep-hdr) - Curated list of deep-learning HDR imaging papers with accompanying code implementations.
- [Disney Dolby Vision Best Practices Guide v1.3.1](https://disneymasteringspecs.s3.amazonaws.com/Disney_Dolby_Vision_Best_Practices_v1_3_1_FINAL_8b61d1a34c.pdf) - Official Disney mastering spec detailing Dolby Vision L0/L1/L2/L5/L6/L8 metadata layers and trim requirements per content type.
- [EBU R 153 — Parameters for Live Contribution of UHD/HDR](https://tech.ebu.ch/docs/r/r153.pdf) - EBU recommendation defining constraints for live UHD/HDR contribution links, complementing R 154's delivery parameters.
- [EBU R 153: Live UHD/HDR Contribution Parameters](https://tech.ebu.ch/publications/r153) - EBU recommendation defining parameters for live UHD-HDR content exchange between production partners.
- [EBU R 154 — Parameters for UHD/HDR Programme Delivery](https://tech.ebu.ch/files/live/sites/tech/files/shared/r/r154.pdf) - EBU recommendation setting required parameters for UHD and HDR programme delivery, referencing ITU-R BT.2100 and BT.2408 practice.
- [EBU R 154: UHD/HDR File Exchange Parameters](https://tech.ebu.ch/files/live/sites/tech/files/shared/r/r154v1_0.pdf) - EBU recommendation defining file-based exchange parameters for UHD/HDR content between production partners.
- [EBU Tech 3373 — HLG UHDTV Colour Bar Test Patterns](https://tech.ebu.ch/publications/tech3373) - Specification and downloadable TIFF/v210 HLG colour bar patterns for validating HDR production pipelines and 2SI signal paths.
- [FOSDEM 2023: 4K HDR Video with AV1 — A Reality Check](https://archive.fosdem.org/2023/schedule/event/om_av1) - FOSDEM Open Media devroom talk covering HDR encoder challenges and signal-conformance/evaluation testing strategies for AV1+HDR content.
- [Frequently Asked Questions on High Dynamic Range and Hybrid Log-Gamma](https://downloads.bbc.co.uk/rd/pubs/papers/HDR/BBC_HDRTV_FAQ.pdf) - BBC R&D guide answering common questions about HDR and HLG technology. Technical reference for HDR implementation.
- [HbbTV: Standards in the UHD Streaming Backend](https://www.hbbtv.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/04/HbbTV-UHD-streaming.pdf) - HbbTV webinar deck on signalling HLG10, PQ10 and dynamic-metadata HDR in CMAF, DASH and HLS backends for UHD services.
- [HDR Toolbox](https://github.com/banterle/HDR_Toolbox) - MATLAB/Octave toolbox with a broad collection of tone mapping operators (Reinhard, Durand, Mertens, etc.), widely cited in academia.
- [ITU-R BT.2100-3 — Image Parameter Values for HDR-TV](https://www.itu.int/dms_pubrec/itu-r/rec/bt/R-REC-BT.2100-3-202502-I!!PDF-E.pdf) - Latest (Feb 2025) official ITU-R recommendation defining PQ and HLG transfer functions and HDR-TV image parameters for production/exchange.
- [ITU-R BT.2408-7: Guidance for HDR Operational Practices](https://www.itu.int/dms_pub/itu-r/opb/rep/R-REP-BT.2408-7-2023-PDF-E.pdf) - Latest ITU guidance document for PQ/HLG HDR production per BT.2100, 2023 revision.
- [ITU-R BT.2408-9 — Operational practices for HDR television production](https://www.itu.int/dms_pub/itu-r/opb/rep/R-REP-BT.2408-9-2026-PDF-E.pdf) - 2026 edition of the ITU report giving practical guidance for HDR production with PQ and HLG, including reference levels and SDR/HDR conversion practice.
- [Netflix Dolby Vision HDR Mastering Guidelines](https://partnerhelp.netflixstudios.com/hc/en-us/articles/360000599948-Dolby-Vision-HDR-Mastering-Guidelines) - Official Netflix delivery specification for Dolby Vision mastering, covering DV 4.0/2.9 profile requirements and IMF packaging.
- [PQ and HLG Transfer Functions for HDR (Venera Technologies)](https://www.veneratech.com/blogs/hdr-transfer-functions-hlg-pq) - QC-vendor explainer on scene-referred HLG vs display-referred PQ transfer functions, tone-mapping implications, and correct signalling in delivery files.
- [Report ITU-R BT.2408-5 — Operational Practices for HDR TV Production](https://www.itu.int/dms_pub/itu-r/opb/rep/R-REP-BT.2408-5-2022-PDF-E.pdf) - Official ITU-R guidance report on HDR TV production operational practices: gamma/exposure handling, best-practice workflows.
- [Subjective Comparison of Three Tone Mapping Algorithms for HDR-to-SDR](https://www.mdpi.com/2079-9292/14/12/2428) - 2025 peer-reviewed paper comparing clip, Reinhard, and Hable-class tone mapping operators for HDR-to-SDR conversion.
- [tonemaplib](https://github.com/abhinaukumar/tonemaplib) - Python library for video-focused HDR tone mapping, including a Reinhard02 implementation.
- [Unsupervised HDR Tone Mapping via Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/html/2303.07327) - TCSVT 2023 academic paper with code (UnCLTMO) on unsupervised video HDR tone mapping with temporal consistency.

### Closed Captioning & Subtitling Standards

- [608toVTT Conversion Note](https://dvcs.w3.org/hg/text-tracks/raw-file/default/608toVTT/608toVTT.html) - W3C technical note on best practices for converting CEA-608/708 caption features into WebVTT.
- [ADM-OSC — ADM object metadata over OSC](https://immersive-audio-live.github.io/ADM-OSC) - Open spec and tools mapping ADM (Audio Definition Model) object metadata onto OSC for live/broadcast immersive audio interop.
- [bbc/ttml-validator](https://github.com/bbc/ttml-validator) - Validates TTML documents against constraint sets including BBC Subtitle Guidelines, IMSC Text, EBU-TT-D, and DAPT profiles.
- [Demuxed Podcast Ep.3: Accessibility for the Future](https://www.heavybit.com/library/podcasts/demuxed/ep-3-accessibility-for-the-future) - Demuxed podcast episode discussing captioning, screen-reader support, and accessibility standards with an SSB BART Group accessibility lead.
- [EBU Tech 3350: EBU-TT Part 1 Subtitling Format Definition](https://tech.ebu.ch/docs/tech/tech3350v1_1.pdf) - EBU's archive/interchange subtitling format definition, sibling spec to EBU-TT-D.
- [EBU Tech 3380: EBU-TT-D Specification](https://tech.ebu.ch/docs/tech/tech3380.pdf) - Official EBU technical specification document for EBU-TT-D subtitle/caption distribution format.
- [EBU Tech 3380: EBU-TT-D Subtitling Distribution Format](https://tech.ebu.ch/publications/tech3380) - EBU's TTML-based subtitling distribution format spec designed for DASH/OTT delivery.
- [EBU-TT-D and IMSC1 Convergence (IBC2015)](https://tech.ebu.ch/docs/events/IBC2015/EBU-TT-D_and_IMSC.pdf) - EBU/W3C technical paper documenting the convergence of EBU-TT-D and IMSC1 subtitle standards.
- [EBU-TT-D XSD](https://github.com/ebu/ebu-tt-d-xsd) - Official EBU-TT-D XML Schema, validates against EBU Tech 3380 subtitle standard.
- [ebu2srt](https://github.com/y-muller/ebu2srt) - Simple converter from EBU-TT-D to SRT subtitle format.
- [ETSI EN 300 743: DVB Subtitling Systems](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_en/300700_300799/300743/01.06.01_20/en_300743v010601a.pdf) - Official DVB bitmap subtitling standard covering region/CLUT/object composition for broadcast PES-based subtitle delivery.
- [FAB ESUB-XF Specification (EBU-STL Successor)](https://www.fab-online.com/pdf/ESUB-XF.pdf) - European Subtitle Exchange Format spec mapping legacy EBU-STL (Tech 3264) into XML.
- [FCC Closed Captioning Rules for Television](https://www.fcc.gov/consumers/guides/closed-captioning-television) - FCC guide to the four 47 CFR §79.1 caption quality standards - accurate, synchronous, complete and properly placed - plus exemptions and complaint procedures.
- [FCC Public Notice DA-21-469: Caption Quality Best Practices](https://docs.fcc.gov/public/attachments/DA-21-469A1.pdf) - FCC public notice detailing caption quality best practices for programmers and live captioners, including latency and accuracy expectations.
- [hls.js CEA-608 Parser](https://github.com/video-dev/hls.js/blob/master/src/utils/cea-608-parser.ts) - Production-grade TypeScript CEA-608 caption parser embedded in hls.js, widely deployed.
- [IMSC 1.2 — TTML Profiles for Internet Media Subtitles and Captions](https://www.w3.org/TR/ttml-imsc1.2) - Latest stable W3C spec text for IMSC 1.2, the base standard behind most subtitle delivery in streaming.
- [IMSC HRM Online Validator](https://hrm.sandflow.com) - Hosted web tool that validates TTML/IMSC subtitle documents against the IMSC Hypothetical Render Model, flagging documents too complex to render in real time.
- [IMSC Text Profile 1.3 (W3C)](https://www.w3.org/TR/ttml-imsc1.3) - W3C specification defining the IMSC 1.3 TTML2 text profile for worldwide subtitle/caption exchange, with validation and presentation requirements.
- [Netflix Timed Text Style Guides](https://partnerhelp.netflixstudios.com/hc/en-us/sections/22463232153235-Timed-Text-Style-Guides) - Netflix's official hub of subtitle/caption style guide requirements and per-language templates, an industry-standard captioning reference.
- [pycaption](https://pypi.org/project/pycaption/0.4.4) - PBS-maintained Python library reading and writing DFXP/TTML/SAMI/SCC/SRT/WebVTT caption formats.
- [SMPTE EG 43: System Implementation of CEA-708 and CEA-608 Closed Captioning](https://pub.smpte.org/latest/eg43/eg0043-2009.pdf) - Freely available SMPTE engineering guideline explaining 608/708 caption carriage across ATSC A/53 and SCTE 20, with ST 334-1/-2 CDP mapping.
- [SMPTE ST 2052-1:2010 Timed Text Format](https://pub.smpte.org/doc/st2052-1/20101203-pub/st2052-1-2010.pdf) - Free official SMPTE-TT specification for FCC safe-harbor caption/subtitle interchange, full PDF.
- [stl2srt](https://github.com/yanncoupin/stl2srt) - CLI tool converting EBU STL and TT (W3C/SMPTE/EBU variants) subtitle formats to SRT.
- [subtitles (PHP)](https://github.com/mantas-done/subtitles) - General-purpose PHP subtitle format converter that enforces SCC line/character limits.
- [TTML Live Extensions Module](https://w3c.github.io/tt-module-live/tt-live-1/spec/tt-live.html) - W3C spec for live TTML/EBU-TT-Live subtitle streaming extensions, niche companion to IMSC.
- [TTML to WebVTT Mapping (W3C)](https://github.com/w3c/ttml-webvtt-mapping) - Official W3C spec repository defining mapping between TTML and WebVTT caption formats.
- [ttml2ssa](https://github.com/Paco8/ttml2ssa) - Converts TTML/DFXP/VTT/SRT subtitle formats (as used by Netflix, HBO, Disney+, Prime) into SRT/SSA formats.
- [ttml2vtt](https://github.com/johanberonius/ttml2vtt) - Single-file XSLT stylesheet converting TTML/DFXP captions to WebVTT, no dependencies beyond xsltproc.
- [W3C IMSC Test Suite](https://github.com/w3c/imsc-tests) - Official IMSC1 conformance test suite; several tests double as EBU-TT-D conformance tests.
- [W3C TimedText Wiki: Efforts and Specifications Index](https://www.w3.org/wiki/TimedText/EffortsAndSpecifications) - Community-maintained index mapping the lineage and relationships of TTML, IMSC, EBU-TT-D, SMPTE-TT and WebVTT along with known implementations.
- [w3c/imsc (imscJS & imscHRM)](https://github.com/w3c/imsc) - Reference repo for the IMSC TTML profiles, hosting imscJS (web renderer/polyfill) and imscHRM (Hypothetical Render Model validator).

### DRM & Content Protection Standards

- [CENC vs CBCS: Common Encryption Schemes for Streaming](https://www.forasoft.com/learn/ott/articles-ott/cenc-ctr-cbcs-common-encryption) - Explainer comparing cenc full-block AES-CTR with cbcs 1-of-10 NAL pattern encryption, including a DRM-per-scheme compatibility matrix and the case for single-cbcs assets.
- [Common Encryption - ISO/IEC 23001-7](https://iso.org/standard/84637.html) - Defines encryption of media supported for receiving devices.
- [CTA-5001-C: WAVE Content Specification](https://www.cta.tech/media/41ehiilm/cta-5001-e-final.pdf) - Official CTA WAVE spec mandating cenc/cbcs CMAF encryption schemes for interoperable streaming devices.
- [DASH-IF CPIX — Content Protection Information Exchange Format](https://dashif.org/CPIX) - Living specification (v2.4) defining the XML document format for exchanging content keys and DRM signaling between key servers, packagers and encoders.
- [dash-test-vectors](https://github.com/petegriffin/dash-test-vectors) - DASH manifest variants over the same CENC-encrypted segments, illustrating DRM-agnostic segment design.
- [Dashif Content Protection List](https://dashif.org/identifiers/content_protection) - A list of content protection schemes recognized by the DASH Industry Forum for securing DASH streaming content.
- [Diving into the Depths of Widevine L3 (Neodyme)](https://neodyme.io/en/blog/widevine_l3) - Deep security research write-up on Widevine L3 CDM internals: Frida hooking and Qiling/Unicorn emulation of OEMCrypto whitebox cryptography.
- [EME HDCP Version Registry](https://www.w3.org/TR/eme-hdcp-version-registry) - W3C companion registry defining HDCP policy strings used with EME's getStatusForPolicy() method.
- [Encrypted Media](https://github.com/w3c/encrypted-media) - W3C specification for Encrypted Media Extensions (EME) API enabling playback of protected content in web browsers.
- [Encrypted Media Extensions (EME) — W3C Working Draft](https://www.w3.org/TR/encrypted-media-2) - Official W3C spec for EME, the browser API for DRM-protected playback control (MediaKeys, encryptionScheme detection, getStatusForPolicy).
- [ETSI TS 103 799 — CPIX Publicly Available Specification](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/103700_103799/103799/01.01.01_60/ts_103799v010101p.pdf) - CPIX published as a formal ETSI standard, giving a normative citation for key/DRM information exchange between packagers and key servers.
- [Formal Security Analysis of Widevine through the W3C EME Standard (USENIX Security '24)](https://www.usenix.org/system/files/usenixsecurity24-delaune.pdf) - Peer-reviewed TAMARIN formal model of the EME + Widevine license protocol across CDM versions 3.1.0-16.1.0, identifying protocol-level weaknesses.
- [HbbTV Reference Application](https://github.com/HbbTV-Association/ReferenceApplication) - MIT-licensed reference DASH player with PlayReady/Marlin/ClearKey test material including cbcs streams, from the official HbbTV Association.
- [HDCP on HDMI Specification Rev 2.3](https://www.digital-cp.com/sites/default/files/specifications/HDCP%20on%20HDMI%20Specification%20Rev2_3.pdf) - Official Digital Content Protection LLC specification covering HDCP authentication, revocation, and encryption over HDMI.
- [HDCP Specifications Index](https://www.digital-cp.com/hdcp-specifications) - Official hub for all HDCP specification variants (HDMI, DisplayPort, HDBaseT, WHDI) plus errata, from the licensing authority.
- [MovieLabs Specification for Enhanced Content Protection v1.4](https://movielabs.com/ngvideo/MovieLabs_ECP_Spec_v1.4.pdf) - The studio-mandated security target for premium content: hardware root of trust, secure media pipeline, forensic watermarking, sample-variant binding and revocation requirements.
- [MPEG-B Part 7: Common Encryption (CENC) overview](https://www.mpeg.org/standards/MPEG-B/7) - MPEG's own free overview page for ISO/IEC 23001-7 Common Encryption, explaining cenc/cbc1/cens/cbcs schemes.
- [PlayReady — Content Encryption Modes (official docs)](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/playready/packaging/content-encryption-modes) - Official Microsoft doc detailing PlayReady's cenc/cbcs content encryption mode support.
- [PlayReady — License and Policies (official docs)](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/playready/overview/license-and-policies) - Official Microsoft technical documentation on PlayReady license/challenge flow, Compliance Rules, and XMR rights spec.
- [pymp4decrypt](https://github.com/truedread/pymp4decrypt) - Python CENC MP4 decrypter extending beardypig's parser with extra Common Encryption box support.
- [W3C ISO CENC Stream Format for EME](https://www.w3.org/TR/eme-stream-mp4) - Informative W3C document bridging CENC ISO-BMFF encryption to browser EME usage.

### Industry Forums & Standards Bodies

- [AIMS Alliance (Alliance for IP Media Solutions)](https://aimsalliance.org) - Industry alliance driving adoption of open IP standards (SMPTE ST 2110, NMOS) for professional media infrastructure interoperability.
- [Awesome Broadcasting](https://github.com/ebu/awesome-broadcasting) - A curated list of amazingly awesome open source resources related to broadcast technologies.
- [CTA WAVE Project](https://cta.tech/Resources/Standards/WAVE-Project) - The WAVE (Web Application Video Ecosystem) Project, hosted by the Consumer Technology Association (CTA)®, aims to improve how internet-delivered commercial video is handled on consumer electronics devices and to make it easier for content creators to distribute video to those devices.
- [CTA WAVE Technical Working Group](https://github.com/cta-wave/technical-working-group) - Live issue tracker and spec repo showing real working-group deliberation on WAVE/HbbTV/ATSC interoperability.
- [Digital TV Group (DTG)](https://dtg.org.uk) - UK broadcast industry body publishing the D-Book specification for Freeview/Freeview HD/FreeSat/YouView interoperability.
- [DVB Project](https://dvb.org) - Digital Video Broadcasting Project - industry consortium (ETSI/CENELEC/EBU) developing DVB-DASH and broadcast standards.
- [EBU Evaluations of Multichannel Audio Codecs](https://tech.ebu.ch/docs/tech/tech3324.pdf) - EBU technical document evaluating and comparing multichannel audio codec standards for broadcast and streaming applications.
- [HbbTV Association](https://www.hbbtv.org/overview) - Pan-European hybrid broadcast/broadband TV standards body covering connected TV/STB specs, absorbed Open IPTV Forum and Smart TV Alliance.
- [IAB Tech Lab](https://iabtechlab.com) - Non-profit standards consortium behind VAST, VPAID, and OM SDK video ad delivery/measurement standards used across web, CTV, and mobile.
- [MovieLabs 2030 Vision](https://movielabs.com/the-2030-vision) - Major studio-backed initiative defining cloud-native, software-defined media production workflows, ontology, and security architecture for the future of content creation.
- [Official Registration Authority for the ISOBMFF family of standards](https://mp4ra.org) - Official registry and authority for managing code registrations and technical definitions within the ISO Base Media File Format family of standards.
- [SCTE Digital Video Subcommittee](https://account.scte.org/information-page-index/digital-video-subcommittee-dvs) - Cable industry standards subcommittee for digital video/audio delivery, coordinating with ATSC, CTA, and SMPTE.
- [SRT Alliance](https://www.srtalliance.org) - Home page for the SRT protocol.
- [Streaming Video Technology Alliance (SVTA)](https://www.svta.org/about-the-svta) - Making Streaming Video Better Streaming video is exploding in popularity. Consumers are watching more video online across a myriad of devices. But, the streaming experiences, across providers, can be wildly different from each other which ultimately hurts adoption.
- [SVTA Labs](https://labs.svta.org) - Streaming Video Technology Alliance GitHub org hosting spec implementations (Common Media Library, LiveSim2, Sandbox) under MIT license.
- [Ultra HD Forum](https://uhdf.svta.org) - Industry group publishing deployment guidelines for HDR, HFR, NGA, and wide color gamut video technologies.

### Specs & Standards

- [CMAF Specification (ISO/IEC 23000-19:2018)](https://www.iso.org/standard/71975.html) - The Common Media Application Format (CMAF) standard defines a container format (based on fragmented MP4) for segmented media delivery that can be used for both HLS and DASH. Understanding CMAF is key to creating streams that work across different streaming protocols with a single media file format.
- [DASH-IF IOP v3.2 Change Summary (vs v3.1)](https://dashif.org/docs/DASH-IF-IOP-v3.2-diff-3.1.pdf) - Document detailing changes between DASH-IF Interoperability Points versions 3.1 and 3.2 for implementers.
- [Encrypted Media Extensions (W3C Recommendation)](https://www.w3.org/TR/encrypted-media) - The W3C specification for Encrypted Media Extensions (EME), which defines a browser API for playback of encrypted content using content decryption modules (CDMs).
- [How Do I Become an ANSI Member](https://www.ansi.org/membership/how_to_join/how_3) - Information about joining ANSI, the American National Standards Institute, which develops and maintains technical standards across various industries.
- [ISO/IEC 14496-12 (MPEG-4 Part 12) ISO Base Media File Format](https://www.iso.org/standard/74428.html) - The ISO base media file format standard (used by MP4, MOV, etc.), defining the general structure for time-based media files. It's a foundation for MP4 and fragmented MP4 (CMAF).
- [Matroska Specification](https://www.matroska.org) - The open specification for the Matroska multimedia container, which is the basis for MKV files. It defines how video, audio, subtitle, and metadata tracks are stored in a single file and is fully open and extensible.
- [Media Source Extensions (W3C Recommendation)](https://www.w3.org/TR/media-source) - The W3C specification for Media Source Extensions (MSE), an API that allows JavaScript to feed media to the HTML5 video or audio tag for adaptive streaming.
- [MovieLabs Production Technology Specifications](https://movielabs.com/production-specs) - Concrete specification documents (Ontology for Media Creation, Media Creation Cloud, security architecture) supporting the 2030 Vision for software-defined media supply chains.
- [Society of Motion Picture and Television Engineers (SMPTE)](https://www.smpte.org) - An organization that develops and maintains standards for cinema and broadcast, including many video formats (e.g., SMPTE ST 2110 for IP video, HDR formats, etc.).
- [Ultra HD Forum Guidelines](https://uhdf.svta.org/guidelines) - Industry guidelines produced by the Ultra HD Forum, covering end-to-end workflows for Ultra HD content (4K, HDR, Wide Color Gamut, Next-Gen Audio) including distribution and streaming considerations. These guidelines help ensure interoperability and best practices for advanced video formats in streaming and broadcast.

#### MPEG & Forums

- [151st MPEG Meeting Report](https://www.mpeg.org/151th-meeting-of-mpeg) - Official MPEG.org meeting report summarizing outcomes and calls-for-proposals from the 151st MPEG (ISO/IEC JTC1/SC29) meeting.
- [Inside MPEG's Ambitious Plan to Launch 3 Video Codecs in 2020](https://www.streamingmedia.com/Articles/Editorial/Featured-Articles/Inside-MPEGs-Ambitious-Plan-to-Launch-3-Video-Codecs-in-2020-134694.aspx) - The pace of innovation is getting faster and the demands on video codecs are getting greater. MPEG's three-part plan answers questions of royalties, licensing, and computational efficiency. Meet VVC, MPEG-5 Part 1 (EVC), and MPEG-5 Part 2 (LCEVC).
- [ISO/IEC JTC 1/SC 29 Committee Page](https://www.iso.org/committee/45316.html) - Official ISO committee catalog and ballot status page for all MPEG coding standards (SC 29).
- [Moving Picture Experts Group (MPEG)](https://mpeg.chiariglione.org) - The official forum that develops international standards for compression, decompression, processing and coded representation of moving pictures, audio, and their combination (e.g., MPEG-2, MPEG-4, DASH).
- [MPEG About](https://www.mpegstandards.org/about-mpeg) - Overview page providing information about the Motion Picture Experts Group organization and its role in developing multimedia standards.
- [MPEG Basics (Chiariglione)](https://mpeg.chiariglione.org/mpeg-basics.html) - Explanation of MPEG standardization process and organizational structure by MPEG founder Leonardo Chiariglione.
- [MPEG home page](https://www.mpeg.org/about-mpeg) - Official website of MPEG, the international standardization body responsible for multimedia compression and streaming standards.
- [MPEG Immersive Video (MIV) Documents](https://mpeg-miv.org/index.php/mpeg-documents) - Public numbered MPEG WG04 N-documents including test models and common test conditions for immersive video coding.
- [MPEG Meetings](https://www.mpegstandards.org/meetings) - Directory of MPEG organization meetings where standards development, voting, and technical discussions take place.
- [MPEG: What Happened?](https://www.streamingmedia.com/Articles/ReadArticle.aspx?ArticleID=141678) - At the end of last month, MPEG co-founder Leonardo Chiariglione announced the 'MPEG is closed.' That's not quite true, but it is undergoing a reorganization. So what does that mean for the organization and the new codec standards it is bringing out this year?
- [Recent Standard Development Activities on Video Coding for Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2105.12653) - Academic paper documenting the MPEG Video Coding for Machines (VCM) ad-hoc group's standardization process.
- [Video Coding Experts Group (VCEG)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Video_Coding_Experts_Group) - Overview of ITU-T's Video Coding Experts Group and its joint work with MPEG (JVET) developing codecs beyond VVC.

#### Official Specs

- [2025 SMPTE Standards Round-Up](https://www.smpte.org/blog/2025-smpte-standards-round-up) - Official SMPTE blog summarizing the year's standards development activity across the organization.
- [AMWA IS-04 — NMOS Discovery and Registration Specification](https://specs.amwa.tv/is-04) - Canonical rendered spec for NMOS registration and query APIs used to discover senders/receivers in SMPTE ST 2110 IP production networks.
- [AMWA IS-05 — NMOS Device Connection Management Specification](https://specs.amwa.tv/is-05) - Spec for configuring sender/receiver connections in NMOS-managed IP video networks, sharing the IS-04 data model.
- [ANSI/SCTE 224-2021: Event Scheduling and Notification Interface (ESNI)](https://account.scte.org/documents/3679/ANSI_SCTE_224_2021.pdf) - Official SCTE-224 spec PDF defining the out-of-band scheduling/policy API complementing SCTE-35's in-stream ad-signaling marks.
- [ATSC A/342-3:2025 — MPEG-H Audio System](https://www.atsc.org/wp-content/uploads/2025/11/A342-3-2025-10-MPEG-H-System.pdf) - 2025 revision of the ATSC 3.0 MPEG-H audio system specification, covering DRC target loudness fields, mae_LoudnessCompensationData and BS.1770-5 alignment.
- [AV1 Bitstream & Decoding Specification (1.0.0 with Errata 1)](https://aomediacodec.github.io/av1-spec/av1-spec.pdf) - The official bitstream and decoding process specification for the AOMedia Video 1 (AV1) codec. Defines how AV1 video is encoded bit-by-bit and decoded.
- [AVC/HEVC in ISOBMFF - ISO/IEC 14496-15](https://www.iso.org/standard/83336.html) - Defines the carriage of AVC/HEVC in ISOBMFF.
- [CTA-5004: Common Media Client Data (CMCD) Specification](https://cdn.cta.tech/cta/media/media/resources/standards/pdfs/cta-5004-final.pdf) - The canonical CTA specification defining CMCD keys and the HTTP header/query transport modes players use to report playback telemetry to CDNs.
- [CTA-5006: Common Media Server Data (CMSD) Specification](https://cdn.cta.tech/cta/media/media/resources/standards/pdfs/cta-5006-final.pdf) - Normative spec for CMSD-Static and CMSD-Dynamic response headers, letting servers and CDNs return per-object telemetry and hints to players.
- [CTA-WAVE Common Media Server Data (CMSD)](https://github.com/cta-wave/common-media-server-data) - CTA-WAVE specification repo for Common Media Server Data (CMSD), the server-to-client counterpart of CMCD.
- [draft-lcurley-moq-hang — MoQ media layer specification](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/draft-lcurley-moq-hang) - Draft defining "hang", the media/catalog layer on top of MoQ transport describing tracks, groups and frame packaging for live A/V.
- [draft-pantos-hls-rfc8216bis-22 — HTTP Live Streaming 2nd Edition](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/draft-pantos-hls-rfc8216bis-22) - Current HLS specification draft (protocol version 13, May 2026) that obsoletes RFC 8216, covering delivery directives and EXT-X-SERVER-CONTROL.
- [EBU ADM Guidelines — BW64 and ADM reference](https://adm.ebu.io/reference/excursions/bw64_and_adm.html) - Official EBU reference explaining BW64 container axml/chna chunks and the ITU-R BS.2076 Audio Definition Model structure for object-based audio.
- [HbbTV Specifications Library](https://www.hbbtv.org/resource-library/specifications) - Canonical index of all HbbTV core specifications and conformance test suites for hybrid broadcast-broadband TV applications.
- [IAMF — Immersive Audio Model and Formats Specification](https://aomediacodec.github.io/iamf) - AOMedia's specification for immersive/scene-based audio delivery, with v1.1.0 approved (base-enhanced profile) and v2.0.0 in draft.
- [latest HLS Spec](https://tools.ietf.org/html/draft-pantos-hls-rfc8216bis-08) - The official IETF specification document for HTTP Live Streaming, defining the protocol standards and technical requirements.
- [SMPTE ST 2110 — Professional Media over Managed IP Networks](https://www.smpte.org/standards/st2110) - Official SMPTE standard page for ST 2110, the professional media-over-IP suite (2025 Emmy-winning standard with EBU and VSF).
- [SMPTE Standards Quarterly Report, March 2025](https://www.smpte.org/hubfs/SMPTE%20Standards%20Quarterly%20Report%20March%202025.pdf?hsLang=en) - Official SMPTE quarterly report detailing status of in-progress standards including ST 2125/2126.

### Vendors & HDR

- [Dolby Vision Overview (Technical PDF)](https://www.ap-prod.net/Dolby_Vision_Overview.pdf) - Vendor-style technical overview of Dolby Vision mastering workflow covering PQ grading, CMU, and trim metadata.
- [Dolby Vision Processing in the Cloud](https://www.encoding.com/blog/dolby-vision-processing-in-the-cloud) - Technical writeup on cloud-based Dolby Vision processing pipeline architecture from Encoding.com engineering blog.
- [DolbyVisionMuxer](https://github.com/KyleLeMarbe/DolbyVisionMuxer) - PowerShell pipeline gluing dovi_tool, mp4muxer, and FFmpeg to convert MKV to Dolby Vision-compatible MP4.
- [DoViBaker — bake Dolby Vision into clips](https://github.com/erazortt/DoViBaker) - AviSynth plugin to bake Dolby Vision dynamic metadata into a clip, best suited for Profile 7 FEL sources.
- [Getting to Know Dolby Vision HDR, Part 1](https://mixinglight.com/color-grading-tutorials/getting-know-dolby-vision-hdr-part-1) - Technical tutorial covering Dolby Vision terminology, CMU hardware, and PQ grading pipeline for colorists.
- [HDR10, HDR10+, HLG, and Dolby Vision Compared](https://codecalamity.com/hdr-hdr10-hdr10-hlg-and-dolby-vision) - Independent technical blog with encoder-level detail: x265/rav1e metadata args, MaxFALL/MaxCLL syntax examples across HDR formats.
- [hdr_forge](https://github.com/JanKremser/hdr_forge) - CLI tool to extract/inject HDR10, Dolby Vision RPU, and HDR10+ metadata without re-encoding.
- [hlg-tools](https://github.com/wswartzendruber/hlg-tools) - Generates 3D LUTs and performs PQ(HDR10)-to-HLG conversion using Oklab color handling.
- [NAB Panel: The Future of HDR in Cinema](https://www.provideocoalition.com/nab-panel-the-future-of-hdr-in-cinema) - Conference panel writeup from NAB covering HDR mastering and delivery workflow strategy, HDR10 vs Dolby Vision distribution.
- [Single-Layer HDR Video Coding with SDR Backward Compatibility (NCTA)](https://www.nctatechnicalpapers.com/Paper/2017/2017-single-layer-hdr-video-coding-with-sdr-backward-compatibility/download) - Technical paper detailing SL-HDR1 decomposition and reconstruction math, plus 10-bit banding constraints for backward-compatible HDR distribution.

#### Vendor Docs

- [Advanced HDR by Technicolor (SL-HDR1/2/3)](https://advancedhdrbytechnicolor.com) - Vendor home for the SL-HDR family (ETSI TS 103 433), which carries an SDR base layer plus reconstruction metadata for backward-compatible HDR delivery.
- [AOM HDR10+ AV1 Metadata Handling Specification](https://aomediacodec.github.io/av1-hdr10plus) - Alliance for Open Media's official spec for HDR10+ dynamic metadata in AV1: OBU metadata_type, ITU-T T35 payload structure, per-frame metadata rules.
- [Dolby Encoding Engine](https://professional.dolby.com/product/dolby-encoding-engine) - Official Dolby CLI encoder tool for Dolby Vision, AC-4, Dolby Digital Plus/Atmos, and TrueHD with batch and plugin architecture.
- [Dolby Hybrik: Dolby Vision Workflow Docs](https://docs.hybrik.com/tutorials/dolby_vision) - Dolby Hybrik cloud transcoding documentation for Dolby Vision workflow configuration, concrete pipeline/config detail.
- [Dolby Vision Developer Technology Portal](https://professional.dolby.com/content-creation/dolby-vision) - Official Dolby developer documentation for Dolby Vision, covering SDK, encoding pipeline, and profile/level guides.
- [Dolby Vision Editor (official sample)](https://github.com/DolbyLaboratories/dolby-vision-editor) - Official Dolby Laboratories sample app demonstrating Dolby Vision editing via Android MediaCodec API, BSD-3 licensed.
- [Dolby Vision for Content Creators](https://professional.dolby.com/content-creation/dolby-vision-for-content-creators) - Official Dolby workflow documentation covering QC via Metafier tool and HEVC bitstream encoding guidance for Dolby Vision content.
- [Dolby Vision for Games — Pipeline Developer Docs](https://professional.dolby.com/gaming) - Official Dolby developer documentation for the Dolby Vision Real-Time Rendering SDK and Unreal Engine plugin pipeline.
- [Dolby Vision Profile 8/9 FAQ](https://professionalsupport.dolby.com/s/article/Dolby-Vision-Profile-20-FAQ?language=en_US) - Official Dolby FAQ with profile-specific engineering detail on Dolby Vision Profile 8 and 9.
- [Dolby Vision Profiles and Levels Explained](https://professionalsupport.dolby.com/s/article/What-is-Dolby-Vision-Profile?language=en_US) - Official Dolby technical support article breaking down Dolby Vision profiles 0-9, cross-compatible base layers (HDR10/HLG/SDR fallback via VUI metadata).
- [Dolby Vision UHD Blu-ray Authoring Workflow Guide](https://professional.dolby.com/siteassets/pdfs/dolby_vision_uhd_bluray_authoring_workflow.pdf) - Spec-level Dolby authoring document covering base-layer mapping (600/1000 nit FEL) and bitstream verifier tool reference.
- [Dolby.io Media APIs — Developer Tools](https://optiview.dolby.com/docs) - Official Dolby.io reference documentation for Media APIs developer tooling.
- [ETSI TS 103 433-2: SL-HDR2 Single-Layer HDR System](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/103400_103499/10343302/01.02.01_60/ts_10343302v010201p.pdf) - Free full specification for SL-HDR2, a PQ-based HDR system carrying dynamic metadata that enables SDR reconstruction from an HDR distribution signal.
- [HDR Imaging and Hybrid Log-Gamma (ICC)](https://www.color.org/groups/displays/20190215_High_Dynamic_Range_imaging_and_Hybrid_Log-Gamma.pdf) - International Color Consortium technical document on HDR colorimetry and HLG (Hybrid Log-Gamma) implementation.
- [HDR10+ AV1 Metadata Handling Specification](https://quantizationbit.github.io/av1-hdr10plus) - Specification for carrying HDR10+ (ST 2094-40) dynamic metadata in AV1 bitstreams via ITU-T T.35 metadata OBUs.
- [HDR10+ Developer Documentation](https://hdr10plus.org/developer-documentation) - Official HDR10+ developer documentation portal including SDKs and gaming development guidelines.
- [HDR10+ Official FAQ and Certification Overview](https://hdr10plus.org/hdr10-faq) - HDR10+ Technologies' official FAQ explaining the SMPTE ST 2094-40 dynamic metadata basis, scope of the system, and the certification/adopter program.
- [HDR10+ Technologies — Official Site](https://hdr10plus.org) - Official home of HDR10+ (SMPTE ST 2094-40) dynamic metadata: adopter specification access, certification program and ecosystem documentation.
- [Samsung SDC22: HDR10+ SALT and Automatic HDR Video Creation](https://developer.samsung.com/conference/sdc22/sessions/hdr10salt-and-automatic-hdr-video-creations-for-productions) - Samsung Developer Conference session on the SALT SDK for automatic HDR10+ video creation in production workflows.
- [Samsung SDC23: HDR10+ Gaming Session](https://developer.samsung.com/conference/sdc23/sessions/hdr10-gaming) - Samsung Developer Conference technical session on HDR10+ Gaming, covering UE5 plugin and GPU API support.
- [SL-HDR1/2/3 Deployment Across AVC, HEVC and VVC (InterDigital/Philips, SET)](https://set.org.br/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/SET-HDR-presentation-V03-final.pdf) - Technical deck on single-layer HDR systems SL-HDR1/2/3, their carriage in AVC/HEVC/VVC, and adoption in SBTVD, ATSC and DVB.

### Video Codec Specifications

- [APV Codec Specification (IETF, now RFC 9924)](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/draft-lim-apv) - IETF document track for the Advanced Professional Video codec, published as RFC 9924 in February 2026.
- [AV2 Bitstream & Decoding Process Specification v1.0.0](https://av2.aomedia.org/v1.0.0/index.html) - Official released AV2 specification text from the Alliance for Open Media.
- [AV2 Specification (AOMedia)](https://av2.aomedia.org) - Official site for the AV2 video codec specification, released v1.0.0 in 2026, with the full self-contained spec document.
- [AVC/h264 Video Coding Standard - ISO/IEC 14496-10](https://www.iso.org/standard/83529.html) - Defines the compressed video format for AVC/h264.
- [FFV1](https://github.com/FFmpeg/FFV1) - Lossless video codec specification.
- [HDMI 2.1: features, specs and news about the latest HDMI standard](https://www.whathifi.com/advice/what-hdmi-21-everything-you-need-to-know) - The gateway to a super high-definition future is ever-nearing.
- [ISO Base Media File Format Reference Software](https://github.com/MPEGGroup/isobmff) - Reference software implementation for the ISO Base Media File Format, providing code for encoding and decoding multimedia files that conform to the ISOBMFF standard.
- [JPEG AI Becomes an International Standard (JPEG Committee)](https://jpeg.org/items/20250219_press.html) - Official JPEG committee announcement that the learning-based JPEG AI coding standard reached International Standard status, with scope and next steps.
- [MPEG Event message track format for the ISO base media file format - ISO/IEC 23001-18](https://www.iso.org/standard/82529.html) - ISO/IEC 23001-18, the Event Message Track Format specification - defines how timed event messages (emsg) are carried as tracks in ISO base media files.
- [MPEG High Efficiency Image File Format (HEIF)](https://nokiatech.github.io/heif) - MPEG specification for the High Efficiency Image File Format, a standard for storing and managing high-quality images with advanced compression capabilities.
- [MPEG ISOBMFF 'MP4' - ISO/IEC 14496-12](https://www.iso.org/standard/83102.html) - ISO/IEC 14496-12, the ISO Base Media File Format specification - defines the structural 'MP4' container that most modern media files and streaming segments are built on.
- [MPEG point cloud compression](https://mpeg-pcc.org) - Technical specification for MPEG point cloud compression standards used in encoding and decoding three-dimensional point cloud data for efficient storage and transmission.
- [MPEG-5 Standards Overview (EVC & LCEVC)](https://mpeg.chiariglione.org/standards/mpeg-5) - Overview of MPEG-5 Part 1 (EVC) and Part 2 (LCEVC) codec standards with links to specifications.
- [MPEG-H Audio a Next Generation Audio (NGA) codec - ISO/IEC 23008-3](https://www.iso.org/standard/83525.html) - Defines the compressed audio format MPEG-H Audio a Next Generation Audio (NGA) codec.
- [Overview of the Versatile Video Coding (VVC) Standard and its Applications](https://publications.rwth-aachen.de/record/835221/export/hr?ln=en) - Open-access record for the IEEE TCSVT overview paper by Bross et al., the de-facto reference text describing VVC's coding tools and applications.
- [The H.264 Sequence Parameter Set](https://www.cardinalpeak.com/the-h-264-sequence-parameter-set) - This is a follow-up to my World's Smallest H.264 Encoder post. I've received several emails asking about precise details of things in two entities in the H.264 bitstream: the Sequence Parameter Set (SPS) and the Picture Parameter Set (PPS).
- [Video Coding - BBC R&D](https://www.bbc.co.uk/rd/projects/video-coding) - BBC video encoding R&D home page.
- [VP9 Bitstream & Decoding Process Specification v0.6](https://storage.googleapis.com/downloads.webmproject.org/docs/vp9/vp9-bitstream-specification-v0.6-20160331-draft.pdf) - Official Google draft bitstream and decoding process specification for VP9.
